import { Game } from "./src/core/Game.js";

const loadingScreen =
    document.getElementById("loading-screen");

const loadingMessage =
    document.getElementById("loading-message");

const loadingProgress =
    document.getElementById("loading-progress");

function setLoading(
    message,
    progress = null
) {

    if (loadingMessage) {

        loadingMessage.textContent =
            message;

    }

    if (
        loadingProgress &&
        progress !== null
    ) {

        const value =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    100,
                    Number(progress)
                )
            );

        loadingProgress.style.width =
            `${value}%`;

        loadingProgress.setAttribute(
            "aria-valuenow",
            String(value)
        );

    }

}

function hideLoading() {

    if (!loadingScreen) {

        return;

    }

    loadingScreen.classList.add(
        "loading-screen-hidden"
    );

    setTimeout(
        () => {

            loadingScreen.hidden =
                true;

        },
        350
    );

}

function showError(error) {

    console.error(
        "Erreur pendant le démarrage d'ISLAMCRAFT :",
        error
    );

    if (loadingMessage) {

        loadingMessage.textContent =
            "Impossible de charger ISLAMCRAFT.";

    }

    if (loadingScreen) {

        loadingScreen.classList.remove(
            "loading-screen-hidden"
        );

    }

    const existing =
        document.getElementById(
            "startup-error"
        );

    if (existing) {

        existing.remove();

    }

    const errorElement =
        document.createElement(
            "p"
        );

    errorElement.id =
        "startup-error";

    errorElement.className =
        "startup-error";

    errorElement.textContent =
        error?.message ??
        "Une erreur inconnue est survenue.";

    loadingScreen
        ?.querySelector(
            ".loading-content"
        )
        ?.appendChild(
            errorElement
        );

}

async function bootstrap() {

    try {

        setLoading(
            "Initialisation du moteur...",
            10
        );

        const game =
            new Game();

        setLoading(
            "Chargement de la scène...",
            30
        );

        await game.initialize();

        setLoading(
            "Démarrage du jeu...",
            80
        );

        game.start();

        window.ISLAMCRAFT =
            game;

        setLoading(
            "ISLAMCRAFT prêt !",
            100
        );

        hideLoading();

        console.log(
            "ISLAMCRAFT client prêt."
        );

    } catch (error) {

        showError(
            error
        );

    }

}

window.addEventListener(
    "DOMContentLoaded",
    bootstrap,
    {
        once: true
    }
);

export class AnimationLoader {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.animations = new Map();

        this.loading = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AnimationLoader initialisé.");

    }

    async load(key, loader) {

        if (this.animations.has(key)) {

            return this.animations.get(key);

        }

        if (this.loading.has(key)) {

            return this.loading.get(key);

        }

        if (typeof loader !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Loader invalide pour l'animation "${key}".`

            );

        }

        const promise = Promise.resolve()

            .then(() => loader())

            .then(animation => {

                this.animations.set(

                    key,

                    animation

                );

                this.loading.delete(key);

                return animation;

            })

            .catch(error => {

                this.loading.delete(key);

                throw error;

            });

        this.loading.set(key, promise);

        return promise;

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.animations.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        return this.animations.get(key) ?? null;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.animations.delete(key);

    }

    clear() {

        this.animations.clear();

        this.loading.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.animations.size;

    }

}

export class AssetManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.assets = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AssetManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(name, asset) {

        if (!name) {

            return;

        }

        this.assets.set(name, asset);

    }

    has(name) {

        return this.assets.has(name);

    }

    get(name) {

        return this.assets.get(name) ?? null;

    }

    remove(name) {

        this.assets.delete(name);

    }

    clear() {

        this.assets.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.assets.size;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.assets.entries()];

    }

}

export class AudioManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.context = null;

        this.masterVolume = 1;

        this.musicVolume = 1;

        this.sfxVolume = 1;

        this.muted = false;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        if (

            typeof window !== "undefined" &&

            window.AudioContext

        ) {

            this.context = new AudioContext();

        }

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("AudioManager initialisé.");

    }

    async resume() {

        if (

            this.context &&

            this.context.state === "suspended"

        ) {

            await this.context.resume();

        }

    }

    setMasterVolume(volume) {

        this.masterVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setMusicVolume(volume) {

        this.musicVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setSfxVolume(volume) {

        this.sfxVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setMuted(muted) {

        this.muted = Boolean(muted);

    }

    toggleMute() {

        this.muted = !this.muted;

        return this.muted;

    }

    getMasterVolume() {

        return this.masterVolume;

    }

    getMusicVolume() {

        return this.musicVolume;

    }

    getSfxVolume() {

        return this.sfxVolume;

    }

    isMuted() {

        return this.muted;

    }

    getEffectiveVolume(volume) {

        if (this.muted) {

            return 0;

        }

        return volume * this.masterVolume;

    }

    async destroy() {

        if (this.context) {

            await this.context.close();

            this.context = null;

        }

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

export class MusicManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentTrack = null;

        this.playing = false;

        this.volume = 1;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MusicManager initialisé.");

    }

    play(track) {

        if (!this.enabled || !track) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentTrack = track;

        this.playing = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-play",

            track

        );

    }

    stop() {

        if (!this.playing) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-stop",

            this.currentTrack

        );

        this.currentTrack = null;

    }

    pause() {

        if (!this.playing) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-pause",

            this.currentTrack

        );

    }

    resume() {

        if (!this.currentTrack) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-resume",

            this.currentTrack

        );

    }

    setVolume(volume) {

        this.volume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    getCurrentTrack() {

        return this.currentTrack;

    }

    getVolume() {

        return this.volume;

    }

    isPlaying() {

        return this.playing;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

export class SFXManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.volume = 1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("SFXManager initialisé.");

    }

    play(sound) {

        if (!this.enabled || !sound) {

            return;

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:sfx-play",

            {

                sound,

                volume: this.volume

            }

        );

    }

    setVolume(volume) {

        this.volume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    stopAll() {

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:sfx-stop-all"

        );

    }

    getVolume() {

        return this.volume;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

================================================== 
export class CameraCollision {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.minimumDistance = 0.5;

        this.collisionDistance = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("CameraCollision initialisé.");

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    setMinimumDistance(distance) {

        this.minimumDistance = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(distance) || 0

        );

    }

    getMinimumDistance() {

        return this.minimumDistance;

    }

    resolve(position, target) {

        if (!this.enabled || !position || !target) {

            return position;

        }

        const dx = position.x - target.x;

        const dy = position.y - target.y;

        const dz = position.z - target.z;

        const distance = Math.sqrt(

            dx * dx +

            dy * dy +

            dz * dz

        );

        this.collisionDistance = distance;

        if (distance <= this.minimumDistance) {

            return {

                x: target.x,

                y: target.y,

                z: target.z

            };

        }

        return {

            x: position.x,

            y: position.y,

            z: position.z

        };

    }

    getCollisionDistance() {

        return this.collisionDistance;

    }

}

================================================== 
export class CameraController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.target = null;

        this.orbit = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.() ?? null;

        this.target =
            this.game.localPlayer ?? null;

        this.orbit =
            this.game.cameraOrbit ?? null;

        console.log(
            "CameraController initialisé."
        );

    }

    setCamera(camera) {

        this.camera = camera;

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

    }

    setOrbit(orbit) {

        this.orbit = orbit;

    }

    rotate(deltaYaw, deltaPitch) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!this.orbit) {
            return;
        }

        this.orbit.rotate(
            deltaYaw,
            deltaPitch
        );

    }

    zoom(delta) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!this.orbit) {
            return;
        }

        this.orbit.zoom(delta);

    }

    getCamera() {

        return this.camera;

    }

    getTarget() {

        return this.target;

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!this.camera || !this.target) {
            return;
        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(
            "camera:target-updated",
            this.target.position
        );

    }

}

================================================== 
export class CameraFollow {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.target = null;

        this.offset = {

            x: 0,

            y: 5,

            z: 8

        };

        this.followSpeed = 8;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.() ?? null;

        this.target = this.game.localPlayer ?? null;

        console.log("CameraFollow initialisé.");

    }

    setCamera(camera) {

        this.camera = camera;

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

    }

    setOffset(x, y, z) {

        this.offset.x = Number(x) || 0;

        this.offset.y = Number(y) || 0;

        this.offset.z = Number(z) || 0;

    }

    setFollowSpeed(speed) {

        this.followSpeed = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(speed) || 0

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !this.camera ||

            !this.target ||

            !this.target.position

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const targetPosition = this.target.position;

        const factor = Math.min(

            1,

            this.followSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        const desiredX =

            targetPosition.x + this.offset.x;

        const desiredY =

            targetPosition.y + this.offset.y;

        const desiredZ =

            targetPosition.z + this.offset.z;

        this.camera.position.x +=

            (desiredX - this.camera.position.x) *

            factor;

        this.camera.position.y +=

            (desiredY - this.camera.position.y) *

            factor;

        this.camera.position.z +=

            (desiredZ - this.camera.position.z) *

            factor;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

export class CameraOrbit {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.target = null;

        this.distance = 6;

        this.minDistance = 2;

        this.maxDistance = 20;

        this.yaw = 0;

        this.pitch = 0.35;

        this.minPitch = -1.2;

        this.maxPitch = 1.2;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.() ?? null;;

        this.target = this.game.localPlayer ?? null;

        console.log("CameraOrbit initialisé.");

    }

    setCamera(camera) {

        this.camera = camera;

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

    }

    setDistance(distance) {

        this.distance = Math.max(

            this.minDistance,

            Math.min(

                this.maxDistance,

                Number(distance) || this.distance

            )

        );

    }

    setYaw(yaw) {

        this.yaw = Number(yaw) || 0;

    }

    setPitch(pitch) {

        this.pitch = Math.max(

            this.minPitch,

            Math.min(

                this.maxPitch,

                Number(pitch) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    rotate(deltaYaw, deltaPitch) {

        this.yaw += Number(deltaYaw) || 0;

        this.setPitch(

            this.pitch + (Number(deltaPitch) || 0)

        );

    }

    zoom(delta) {

        this.setDistance(

            this.distance + (Number(delta) || 0)

        );

    }

    update() {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !this.camera ||

            !this.target ||

            !this.target.position

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const target = this.target.position;

const cosPitch = Math.cos(this.pitch);
const sinPitch = Math.sin(this.pitch);

/*
 * La caméra reste toujours derrière le joueur.
 * La rotation du joueur détermine donc le yaw
 * de la caméra.
 */
const playerYaw =
    this.target.rotation?.y ?? 0;

const cameraYaw =
    playerYaw - Math.PI;

const cosYaw =
    Math.cos(cameraYaw);

const sinYaw =
    Math.sin(cameraYaw);

        this.camera.position.x =

            target.x +

            this.distance *

            cosPitch *

            sinYaw;

        this.camera.position.y =

            target.y +

            this.distance *

            sinPitch;

        this.camera.position.z =

            target.z +

            this.distance *

            cosPitch *

            cosYaw;
        
        this.camera.lookAt(
    target.x,
    target.y + 0.9,
    target.z
);

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

export class CameraSmoothing {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.positionSpeed = 10;

        this.rotationSpeed = 10;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("CameraSmoothing initialisé.");

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    setPositionSpeed(speed) {

        this.positionSpeed = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(speed) || 0

        );

    }

    setRotationSpeed(speed) {

        this.rotationSpeed = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(speed) || 0

        );

    }

    smoothPosition(current, target, deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled || !current || !target) {

            return current;

        }

        const factor = Math.min(

            1,

            this.positionSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        current.x +=

            (target.x - current.x) * factor;

        current.y +=

            (target.y - current.y) * factor;

        current.z +=

            (target.z - current.z) * factor;

        return current;

    }

    smoothRotation(current, target, deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled || !current || !target) {

            return current;

        }

        const factor = Math.min(

            1,

            this.rotationSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        current.x +=

            (target.x - current.x) * factor;

        current.y +=

            (target.y - current.y) * factor;

        current.z +=

            (target.z - current.z) * factor;

        return current;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

================================================== 
export class CleanupManager {

    constructor() {

        this.tasks = new Set();

    }

    register(task) {

        if (typeof task !== "function") {

            throw new Error("CleanupManager : la tâche doit être une fonction.");

        }

        this.tasks.add(task);

    }

    unregister(task) {

        this.tasks.delete(task);

    }

    cleanup() {

        for (const task of this.tasks) {

            try {

                task();

            } catch (error) {

                console.error("Erreur pendant le nettoyage :", error);

            }

        }

        this.tasks.clear();

    }

    destroy() {

        this.cleanup();

    }

}

await this.touchInput.initialize();


this.cameraTouchInput =

await this.cameraTouchInput.initialize();


this.inputManager =
    new InputManager(this);

        await this.inputManager.initialize();

    stop() {

        if (this.loop) {

            this.loop.stop();

        }

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * INPUT
         */

        if (this.inputManager) {

            this.inputManager.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }


        /*
         * JOUEUR
         */

        if (this.playerController) {
    this.playerController.update(
        deltaTime
    );
    if (
    this.collisionController
) {

    this.collisionController.update(
        deltaTime
    );

}
if (
    this.interactionController
) {

    this.interactionController.update(
        deltaTime
    );

}
}


/*
 * MONDE
 */

if (this.world) {
    this.world.update(
        deltaTime
    );
}


/*
 * ENTITÉS
 */

        if (this.entityManager) {

            this.entityManager.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }


        /*
         * CAMÉRA
         */

        if (this.cameraController) {

            this.cameraController.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

        if (this.cameraOrbit) {

            this.cameraOrbit.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

        if (this.cameraFollow) {

            this.cameraFollow.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }


        /*
         * SCÈNE
         */

        if (this.scene) {

            this.scene.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

    }


    render(interpolation) {

        if (this.renderer) {

            this.renderer.render(
                interpolation
            );

        }

    }

}

export class GameLoop {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.running = false;

        this.lastFrameTime = 0;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.fixedTimeStep = 1 / 60;

        this.accumulator = 0;

        this.maxFrameTime = 0.25;

        this.frameId = null;

        this.frameCount = 0;

        this.fps = 0;

        this.fpsTimer = 0;

        this.loop = this.loop.bind(this);

    }

    start() {

        if (this.running) return;

        this.running = true;

        this.lastFrameTime = performance.now() / 1000;

        this.frameId = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);

    }

    stop() {

        this.running = false;

        if (this.frameId !== null) {

            cancelAnimationFrame(this.frameId);

            this.frameId = null;

        }

    }

    loop() {

        if (!this.running) return;

        const currentTime = performance.now() / 1000;

        let frameTime = currentTime - this.lastFrameTime;

        this.lastFrameTime = currentTime;

        if (frameTime > this.maxFrameTime) {

            frameTime = this.maxFrameTime;

        }

        this.deltaTime = frameTime;

        this.accumulator += frameTime;

        while (this.accumulator >= this.fixedTimeStep) {

            this.game.update(this.fixedTimeStep);

            this.accumulator -= this.fixedTimeStep;

        }

        const interpolation = this.accumulator / this.fixedTimeStep;

        this.game.render(interpolation);

        this.updateFPS(frameTime);

        this.frameId = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);

    }

    updateFPS(deltaTime) {

        this.frameCount++;

        this.fpsTimer += deltaTime;

        if (this.fpsTimer >= 1) {

            this.fps = this.frameCount;

            this.frameCount = 0;

            this.fpsTimer = 0;

        }

    }

    getFPS() {

        return this.fps;

    }

    getDeltaTime() {

        return this.deltaTime;

    }

}

export class GameState {

    constructor() {

        this.started = false;
        this.paused = false;

        this.currentState = "BOOT";

        this.previousState = null;

        this.playerReady = false;

        this.worldReady = false;

        this.networkReady = false;

        this.assetsReady = false;

        this.loadingProgress = 0;

    }

    setState(newState) {

        if (this.currentState === newState) return;

        this.previousState = this.currentState;

        this.currentState = newState;

        console.log(
            `[GameState] ${this.previousState} -> ${this.currentState}`
        );

    }

    getState() {

        return this.currentState;

    }

    is(stateName) {

        return this.currentState === stateName;

    }

    markStarted() {

        this.started = true;

    }

    markPaused(value = true) {

        this.paused = value;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.started && !this.paused;

    }

    setLoadingProgress(percent) {

        this.loadingProgress = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(100, percent)
        );

    }

    getLoadingProgress() {

        return this.loadingProgress;

    }

    setWorldReady(value = true) {

        this.worldReady = value;

    }

    setAssetsReady(value = true) {

        this.assetsReady = value;

    }

    setNetworkReady(value = true) {

        this.networkReady = value;

    }

    setPlayerReady(value = true) {

        this.playerReady = value;

    }

    isFullyReady() {

        return (
            this.worldReady &&
            this.assetsReady &&
            this.playerReady &&
            this.networkReady
        );

    }

}

export class TimeManager {

    constructor() {

        this.startTime = performance.now();

        this.currentTime = this.startTime;

        this.previousTime = this.startTime;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.timeScale = 1;

        this.paused = false;

    }

    update() {

        this.currentTime = performance.now();

        if (this.paused) {

            this.deltaTime = 0;

            this.previousTime = this.currentTime;

            return;

        }

        this.deltaTime =
            ((this.currentTime - this.previousTime) / 1000) *
            this.timeScale;

        this.previousTime = this.currentTime;

        this.elapsedTime += this.deltaTime;

    }

    pause() {

        this.paused = true;

    }

    resume() {

        this.paused = false;

        this.previousTime = performance.now();

    }

    reset() {

        this.startTime = performance.now();

        this.currentTime = this.startTime;

        this.previousTime = this.startTime;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    setTimeScale(scale) {

        this.timeScale = Math.max(0, scale);

    }

    getDeltaTime() {

        return this.deltaTime;

    }

    getElapsedTime() {

        return this.elapsedTime;

    }

    getElapsedMilliseconds() {

        return this.elapsedTime * 1000;

    }

    isPaused() {

        return this.paused;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class EnvironmentRenderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.sky = null;

        this.ground = null;

        this.grid = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene = this.game.scene.getScene();

        this.createGround();

        this.createGrid();

        this.createSky();

        console.log("EnvironmentRenderer initialisé.");

    }

    createGround() {

        const geometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1000, 1000);

        const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x6aa84f

        });

        this.ground = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;

        this.ground.receiveShadow = true;

        this.scene.add(this.ground);

    }

    createGrid() {

        this.grid = new THREE.GridHelper(

            1000,

            100,

            0x666666,

            0x444444

        );

        this.grid.visible = this.game.config.debug.showChunks;

        this.scene.add(this.grid);

    }

    createSky() {

        this.sky = new THREE.Color(

            this.game.config.backgroundColor

        );

        this.scene.background = this.sky;

    }

    update() {

        // Plus tard :
        // - cycle jour / nuit
        // - brouillard dynamique
        // - météo
        // - nuages
        // - ambiance

    }

    setGroundColor(color) {

        this.ground.material.color.set(color);

    }

    setBackground(color) {

        this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(color);

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class FrustumCulling {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.frustum = new THREE.Frustum();

        this.projectionMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();

        this.dynamicObjects = new Set();

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera = this.game.scene.getCamera();

        console.log("FrustumCulling initialisé.");

    }

    register(object) {

        if (!object) return;

        this.dynamicObjects.add(object);

    }

    unregister(object) {

        this.dynamicObjects.delete(object);

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.enabled) return;

        this.projectionMatrix.multiplyMatrices(

            this.camera.projectionMatrix,

            this.camera.matrixWorldInverse

        );

        this.frustum.setFromProjectionMatrix(

            this.projectionMatrix

        );

        for (const object of this.dynamicObjects) {

            if (!object) continue;

            if (!object.geometry) continue;

            if (!object.geometry.boundingSphere) {

                object.geometry.computeBoundingSphere();

            }

            const sphere = object.geometry.boundingSphere.clone();

            sphere.applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);

            object.visible = this.frustum.intersectsSphere(sphere);

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.dynamicObjects.clear();

    }

    setEnabled(value) {

        this.enabled = value;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class InstancingManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.instancedMeshes = new Map();

        this.tempMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene = this.game.scene.getScene();

        console.log("InstancingManager initialisé.");

    }

    createInstancedMesh(id, geometry, material, maxInstances) {

        if (this.instancedMeshes.has(id)) {

            return this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        }

        const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(

            geometry,

            material,

            maxInstances

        );

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        mesh.count = 0;

        this.scene.add(mesh);

        this.instancedMeshes.set(id, mesh);

        return mesh;

    }

    addInstance(id, position, rotation, scale = 1) {

        const mesh = this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        if (!mesh) return -1;

        const index = mesh.count;

        this.tempMatrix.compose(

            position,

            rotation,

            new THREE.Vector3(scale, scale, scale)

        );

        mesh.setMatrixAt(index, this.tempMatrix);

        mesh.count++;

        mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;

        return index;

    }

    clear(id) {

        const mesh = this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        if (!mesh) return;

        mesh.count = 0;

        mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;

    }

    remove(id) {

        const mesh = this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        if (!mesh) return;

        this.scene.remove(mesh);

        mesh.dispose();

        this.instancedMeshes.delete(id);

    }

    clearAll() {

        for (const id of this.instancedMeshes.keys()) {

            this.remove(id);

        }

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Lighting {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.ambientLight = null;

        this.directionalLight = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene = this.game.scene.getScene();

        this.createAmbientLight();

        this.createDirectionalLight();

        console.log("Lighting initialisé.");

    }

    createAmbientLight() {

        this.ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(

            0xffffff,

            1.2

        );

        this.scene.add(this.ambientLight);

    }

    createDirectionalLight() {

        this.directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(

            0xffffff,

            2.0

        );

        this.directionalLight.position.set(

            100,

            150,

            75

        );

        this.directionalLight.castShadow = true;

        this.directionalLight.shadow.mapSize.width = 4096;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.mapSize.height = 4096;

        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.near = 1;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.far = 500;

        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.left = -200;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.right = 200;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.top = 200;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.bottom = -200;

        this.scene.add(this.directionalLight);

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé au futur cycle jour/nuit.
    }

    getSun() {

        return this.directionalLight;

    }

    getAmbient() {

        return this.ambientLight;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class LODManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.objects = new Set();

        this.defaultDistances = {

            HIGH: 35,
            MEDIUM: 80,
            LOW: 180,
            HIDDEN: 300

        };

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera = this.game.scene.getCamera();

        console.log("LODManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(object, options = {}) {

        if (!object) return;

        object.userData.lod = {

            high: options.high ?? this.defaultDistances.HIGH,

            medium: options.medium ?? this.defaultDistances.MEDIUM,

            low: options.low ?? this.defaultDistances.LOW,

            hidden: options.hidden ?? this.defaultDistances.HIDDEN,

            current: "HIGH"

        };

        this.objects.add(object);

    }

    unregister(object) {

        this.objects.delete(object);

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.enabled) return;

        if (!this.camera) return;

        const cameraPosition = this.camera.position;

        for (const object of this.objects) {

            if (!object) continue;

            const distance = cameraPosition.distanceTo(

                object.position

            );

            const lod = object.userData.lod;

            if (!lod) continue;

            if (distance > lod.hidden) {

                object.visible = false;

                lod.current = "HIDDEN";

            }

            else {

                object.visible = true;

                if (distance <= lod.high) {

                    lod.current = "HIGH";

                }

                else if (distance <= lod.medium) {

                    lod.current = "MEDIUM";

                }

                else {

                    lod.current = "LOW";

                }

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.objects.clear();

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Renderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.renderer = null;

        this.clock = new THREE.Clock();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({

            antialias: this.game.config.antialias,

            alpha: this.game.config.alpha

        });

        this.renderer.setPixelRatio(

            this.game.config.pixelRatio

        );

        this.renderer.setSize(

            window.innerWidth,

            window.innerHeight

        );

        this.renderer.setClearColor(

            this.game.config.backgroundColor

        );

        this.renderer.shadowMap.enabled =

            this.game.config.shadows;

        this.renderer.shadowMap.type =

            THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;

        document.body.style.margin = "0";

        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

        document.body.appendChild(

            this.renderer.domElement

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "resize",

            this.onResize.bind(this)

        );

        console.log("Renderer initialisé.");

    }

    render() {

        if (

            !this.game.scene ||

            !this.game.scene.scene ||

            !this.game.scene.camera

        ) {

            return;

        }

        this.renderer.render(

            this.game.scene.scene,

            this.game.scene.camera

        );

    }

    onResize() {

        if (

            !this.game.scene ||

            !this.game.scene.camera

        ) {

            return;

        }

        this.game.scene.camera.aspect =

            window.innerWidth /

            window.innerHeight;

        this.game.scene.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();

        this.renderer.setSize(

            window.innerWidth,

            window.innerHeight

        );

    }

    getRenderer() {

        return this.renderer;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class SceneManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.camera = null;

        this.clock = new THREE.Clock();

        this.updatables = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.createScene();

        this.createCamera();

        console.log("SceneManager initialisé.");

    }

    createScene() {

        this.scene = new THREE.Scene();

        this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(
            this.game.config.backgroundColor
        );

        this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(
            this.game.config.backgroundColor,
            100,
            this.game.config.camera.far
        );

    }

    createCamera() {

        this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(

            this.game.config.camera.fov,

            window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight,

            this.game.config.camera.near,

            this.game.config.camera.far

        );

        this.camera.position.set(0, 8, 12);

        this.camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    }

    add(object) {

        if (!object) return;

        this.scene.add(object);

    }

    remove(object) {

        if (!object) return;

        this.scene.remove(object);

    }

    registerUpdatable(system) {

        if (!system) return;

        if (typeof system.update !== "function") return;

        this.updatables.push(system);

    }

    unregisterUpdatable(system) {

        this.updatables = this.updatables.filter(
            item => item !== system
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const system of this.updatables) {

            system.update(deltaTime);

        }

    }

    getScene() {

        return this.scene;

    }

    getCamera() {

        return this.camera;

    }

}

import { LocalPlayer } from "./player/LocalPlayer.js";
import { RemotePlayer } from "./player/RemotePlayer.js";
import { Chest } from "./chest/Chest.js";
import { WorldObject } from "./world/WorldObject.js";
import { InteractiveObject } from "./world/InteractiveObject.js";
import { Collectible } from "./world/Collectible.js";

export class EntityFactory {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("EntityFactory initialisé.");

    }

    create(type, data = {}) {

        switch (type) {

            case "localPlayer":

                return new LocalPlayer(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "remotePlayer":

                return new RemotePlayer(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "chest":

                return new Chest(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "worldObject":

                return new WorldObject(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "interactiveObject":

                return new InteractiveObject(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "collectible":

                return new Collectible(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            default:

                throw new Error(

                    `Type d'entité inconnu : ${type}`

                );

        }

    }

}

export class EntityManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.entities = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("EntityManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(entity) {

        if (!entity) {

            return;

        }

        this.entities.set(

            entity.id,

            entity

        );

    }

    unregister(id) {

        this.entities.delete(id);

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.entities.get(id);

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.entities.has(id);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [

            ...this.entities.values()

        ];

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const entity of this.entities.values()) {

            if (typeof entity.update === "function") {

                entity.update(deltaTime);

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.entities.clear();

    }

}

export class EntityRegistry {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.types = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("EntityRegistry initialisé.");

    }

    register(type, constructor) {

        if (this.types.has(type)) {

            console.warn(

                `Le type "${type}" est déjà enregistré.`

            );

            return;

        }

        this.types.set(

            type,

            constructor

        );

    }

    unregister(type) {

        this.types.delete(type);

    }

    has(type) {

        return this.types.has(type);

    }

    get(type) {

        return this.types.get(type);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.types.keys()];

    }

    clear() {

        this.types.clear();

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Chest {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id = data.id ?? crypto.randomUUID();

        this.position = data.position?.clone() ?? new THREE.Vector3();

        this.rotation = new THREE.Euler();

        this.mesh = null;

        this.isOpen = false;

        this.isActive = true;

        this.canInteract = true;

        this.loot = data.loot ?? null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("Chest initialisé.");

    }

    setMesh(mesh) {

        this.mesh = mesh;

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(this.position);

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(this.rotation);

        }

    }

    setPosition(position) {

        this.position.copy(position);

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(position);

        }

    }

    open() {

        if (!this.canInteract || this.isOpen) {

            return false;

        }

        this.isOpen = true;

        this.canInteract = false;

        if (this.game.chestAnimator) {

            this.game.chestAnimator.playOpen(this);

        }

        if (this.game.audioManager?.play) {

            this.game.audioManager.play("chest_open");

        }

        return true;

    }

    close() {

        this.isOpen = false;

        this.canInteract = true;

    }

    destroy() {

        this.isActive = false;

        if (this.mesh && this.game.scene) {

            this.game.scene.remove(this.mesh);

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.isActive) {

            return;

        }

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(this.position);

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(this.rotation);

        }

    }

}

C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\entities\chest\ChestAnimator.js 
================================================== 
export class ChestAnimator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.animations = new Map();

        this.openAngle = -Math.PI / 2;

        this.animationSpeed = 4;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ChestAnimator initialisé.");

    }

    playOpen(chest) {

        if (!chest || !chest.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.animations.set(

            chest.id,

            {

                chest,

                target: this.openAngle,

                finished: false

            }

        );

    }

    playClose(chest) {

        if (!chest || !chest.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.animations.set(

            chest.id,

            {

                chest,

                target: 0,

                finished: false

            }

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const animation of this.animations.values()) {

            const lid =

                animation.chest.mesh.userData.lid;

            if (!lid) {

                continue;

            }

            const current = lid.rotation.x;

            const target = animation.target;

            const next = current +

                (target - current) *

                Math.min(

                    1,

                    deltaTime * this.animationSpeed

                );

            lid.rotation.x = next;

            if (Math.abs(target - next) < 0.01) {

                lid.rotation.x = target;

                animation.finished = true;

            }

        }

        for (const [id, animation] of this.animations) {

            if (animation.finished) {

               this.animations.delete(id);

            }

        }

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChestInteraction {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.interactionDistance = 3;

        this.nearbyChests = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ChestInteraction initialisé.");

    }

    register(chest) {

        if (!chest) {

            return;

        }

        this.nearbyChests.set(

            chest.id,

            chest

        );

    }

    unregister(id) {

        this.nearbyChests.delete(id);

    }

    check(playerPosition) {

        const available = [];

        for (const chest of this.nearbyChests.values()) {

            if (!chest.isActive || chest.isOpen) {

                continue;

            }

            const distance =

                playerPosition.distanceTo(

                    chest.position

                );

            if (distance <= this.interactionDistance) {

                available.push(chest);

            }

        }

        return available;

    }

    interact(playerPosition) {

        const chests = this.check(

            playerPosition

        );

        if (chests.length === 0) {

            return false;

        }

        const chest = chests[0];

        const opened = chest.open();

        if (!opened) {

            return false;

        }

        if (this.game.lootController) {

            this.game.lootController.generateLoot(

                chest

            );

        }

        if (this.game.networkManager?.sendChestOpen) {

            this.game.networkManager.sendChestOpen(

                chest.id

            );

        }

        return true;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Futures améliorations :
        // - touche interaction
        // - mobile bouton
        // - highlight coffre proche
        // - animation prompt

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChestRenderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.materials = {};

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.materials.base = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x8b5a2b

        });

        this.materials.lid = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x6d3f1d

        });

        this.materials.lock = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0xffd700

        });

        console.log("ChestRenderer initialisé.");

    }

    createChest() {

        const group = new THREE.Group();

        const base = this.createBase();
        const lid = this.createLid();
        const lock = this.createLock();

        group.add(base);
        group.add(lid);
        group.add(lock);

        group.userData.base = base;
        group.userData.lid = lid;
        group.userData.lock = lock;

        group.castShadow = true;
        group.receiveShadow = true;

        return group;

    }

    createBase() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            1.4,
            0.8,
            1

        );

        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            this.materials.base

        );

        mesh.position.y = 0.4;

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

    createLid() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            1.4,
            0.25,
            1

        );

        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            this.materials.lid

        );

        mesh.position.y = 0.95;

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

    createLock() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            0.15,
            0.2,
            0.05

        );

        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            this.materials.lock

        );

        mesh.position.set(

            0,
            0.75,
            0.53

        );

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";
import { PlayerModel } from "./PlayerModel.js";

export class LocalPlayer {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id =
            data.id ??
            crypto.randomUUID();

        this.username =
            data.username ??
            "Player";

        this.skin =
            data.skin ??
            "default";

        this.position =
            new THREE.Vector3();

        this.rotation =
            new THREE.Euler();

        this.velocity =
            new THREE.Vector3();

        this.mesh = null;

        this.model = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }


    async initialize() {

        /*
         * Position initiale
         */

        this.position.set(
            0,
            0,
            0
        );


        /*
         * Création du modèle 3D
         */

        this.model =
            new PlayerModel(this.game);

        await this.model.initialize();


        /*
         * Récupération du groupe 3D
         */

        this.setMesh(
            this.model.getMesh()
        );
        this.mesh.scale.set(
    0.85,
    0.85,
    0.85
);


        /*
         * Placement dans la scène
         */

        if (
            this.mesh &&
            this.game.scene
        ) {

            this.game.scene.add(
                this.mesh
            );

        }


        /*
         * Position initiale
         */

        this.mesh?.position.copy(
            this.position
        );

        this.mesh?.rotation.copy(
            this.rotation
        );


        console.log(
            "LocalPlayer initialisé avec modèle 3D."
        );

    }


    setMesh(mesh) {

        this.mesh = mesh;

    }


    setPosition(x, y, z) {

        this.position.set(
            x,
            y,
            z
        );

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(
                this.position
            );

        }

    }


    setRotation(x, y, z) {

        this.rotation.set(
            x,
            y,
            z
        );

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(
                this.rotation
            );

        }

    }


    translate(vector) {

        this.position.add(
            vector
        );

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(
                this.position
            );

        }

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(
                this.position
            );

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(
                this.rotation
            );

        }

    }


    destroy() {

        if (
            this.mesh &&
            this.game.scene
        ) {

            this.game.scene.remove(
                this.mesh
            );

        }

        if (this.model?.group) {

            this.model.group.traverse(
                object => {

                    if (object.geometry) {

                        object.geometry.dispose();

                    }

                    if (object.material) {

                        if (
                            Array.isArray(
                                object.material
                            )
                        ) {

                            object.material.forEach(
                                material =>
                                    material.dispose()
                            );

                        }
                        else {

                            object.material.dispose();

                        }

                    }

                }
            );

        }

        this.mesh = null;
        this.model = null;

    }

}

export class PlayerAnimator {

    constructor(game, playerModel) {

        this.game = game;

        this.playerModel = playerModel;

        this.walkTime = 0;

        this.walkSpeed = 8;

        this.walkAmplitude = 0.7;

        this.currentState = "idle";

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("PlayerAnimator initialisé.");

    }

    setState(state) {

        this.currentState = state;

    }

    update(deltaTime, velocity) {

        if (!this.playerModel) {

            return;

        }

        const speed = velocity.length();

        if (speed > 0.05) {

            this.currentState = "walk";

        } else {

            this.currentState = "idle";

        }

        switch (this.currentState) {

            case "walk":

                this.animateWalk(deltaTime);

                break;

            default:

                this.animateIdle(deltaTime);

                break;

        }

    }

    animateIdle(deltaTime) {

        this.walkTime += deltaTime;

        const offset =

            Math.sin(this.walkTime * 2) * 0.02;

        this.playerModel.group.position.y = offset;

        this.playerModel.leftArm.rotation.x = 0;

        this.playerModel.rightArm.rotation.x = 0;

        this.playerModel.leftLeg.rotation.x = 0;

        this.playerModel.rightLeg.rotation.x = 0;

    }

    animateWalk(deltaTime) {

        this.walkTime += deltaTime * this.walkSpeed;

        const armAngle =

            Math.sin(this.walkTime) * this.walkAmplitude;

        const legAngle =

            Math.sin(this.walkTime) * this.walkAmplitude;

        this.playerModel.leftArm.rotation.x = armAngle;

        this.playerModel.rightArm.rotation.x = -armAngle;

        this.playerModel.leftLeg.rotation.x = -legAngle;

        this.playerModel.rightLeg.rotation.x = legAngle;

    }

}

C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\entities\player\PlayerController.js

import * as THREE from "three";

export class PlayerController {

    constructor(game, player) {

        this.game = game;

        this.player = player;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.moveDirection = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.cameraDirection = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.cameraRight = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.velocity = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.speed = 6;

        this.rotationSpeed = 5;

    }


    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "PlayerController initialisé."
        );

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }


        const input =
            this.game.inputManager?.getMovement?.();


        if (!input) {

            return;

        }


        /*
         * Direction du déplacement.
         */

        this.moveDirection.set(
            0,
            0,
            0
        );


        const camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.();


        if (camera) {

            camera.getWorldDirection(
                this.cameraDirection
            );


            this.cameraDirection.y =
                0;


            this.cameraDirection.normalize();


            this.cameraRight.crossVectors(
                this.cameraDirection,
                new THREE.Vector3(
                    0,
                    1,
                    0
                )
            );


            this.cameraRight.normalize();

        }


        this.moveDirection.addScaledVector(
            this.cameraDirection,
            input.forward
        );


        this.moveDirection.addScaledVector(
            this.cameraRight,
            input.right
        );


        if (
            this.moveDirection.lengthSq() > 0
        ) {

            this.moveDirection.normalize();

        }


        /*
         * Vitesse de déplacement.
         */

        this.velocity.copy(
            this.moveDirection
        ).multiplyScalar(
            this.speed * deltaTime
        );


        /*
         * COLLISIONS
         *
         * Un seul appel au CollisionController.
         */

        const collisionController =
            this.game.collisionController;


        if (
            collisionController
        ) {

            const allowedMovement =
                collisionController.resolveMovement(
                    this.player.position,
                    this.velocity
                );


            this.velocity.copy(
                allowedMovement
            );

        }


        /*
         * Application du déplacement.
         */

        this.player.translate(
            this.velocity
        );


        /*
         * Rotation du personnage.
         */

        if (
            this.moveDirection.lengthSq() >
            0.001
        ) {

            const angle =
                Math.atan2(
                    this.moveDirection.x,
                    this.moveDirection.z
                );


            let angleDifference =
                angle -
                this.player.rotation.y;


            while (
                angleDifference >
                Math.PI
            ) {

                angleDifference -=
                    Math.PI * 2;

            }


            while (
                angleDifference <
                -Math.PI
            ) {

                angleDifference +=
                    Math.PI * 2;

            }


            this.player.rotation.y +=
                angleDifference *
                Math.min(
                    1,
                    deltaTime *
                    this.rotationSpeed
                );

        }


        /*
         * Animation.
         */

        if (
            this.game.playerAnimator
        ) {

            this.game.playerAnimator.update(
                deltaTime,
                this.velocity
            );

        }


        /*
         * Synchronisation réseau.
         */

        if (
            this.game.networkManager
                ?.sendPlayerMovement
        ) {

            this.game.networkManager.sendPlayerMovement({

                position:
                    this.player.position,

                rotation:
                    this.player.rotation

            });

        }

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class PlayerInterpolation {

    constructor(game, remotePlayer) {

        this.game = game;

        this.remotePlayer = remotePlayer;

        this.positionBuffer = [];

        this.rotationBuffer = [];

        this.interpolationDelay = 100;

        this.maxBufferSize = 20;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("PlayerInterpolation initialisé.");

    }

    pushSnapshot(snapshot) {

        if (!snapshot) {

            return;

        }

        this.positionBuffer.push({

            time: snapshot.time,

            position: new THREE.Vector3(

                snapshot.position.x,

                snapshot.position.y,

                snapshot.position.z

            )

        });

        this.rotationBuffer.push({

            time: snapshot.time,

            rotation: new THREE.Euler(

                snapshot.rotation.x,

                snapshot.rotation.y,

                snapshot.rotation.z

            )

        });

        while (this.positionBuffer.length > this.maxBufferSize) {

            this.positionBuffer.shift();

        }

        while (this.rotationBuffer.length > this.maxBufferSize) {

            this.rotationBuffer.shift();

        }

    }

    update(serverTime) {

        if (this.positionBuffer.length < 2) {

            return;

        }

        const renderTime = serverTime - this.interpolationDelay;

        let previous = null;
        let next = null;

        for (let i = 0; i < this.positionBuffer.length - 1; i++) {

            const a = this.positionBuffer[i];
            const b = this.positionBuffer[i + 1];

            if (renderTime >= a.time && renderTime <= b.time) {

                previous = a;
                next = b;
                break;

            }

        }

        if (!previous || !next) {

            return;

        }

        const alpha =

            (renderTime - previous.time) /

            (next.time - previous.time);

        this.remotePlayer.position.lerpVectors(

            previous.position,

            next.position,

            alpha

        );

        this.remotePlayer.mesh.position.copy(

            this.remotePlayer.position

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.positionBuffer.length = 0;

        this.rotationBuffer.length = 0;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class PlayerModel {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.group = null;

        this.body = null;

        this.head = null;

        this.leftArm = null;

        this.rightArm = null;

        this.leftLeg = null;

        this.rightLeg = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.group = new THREE.Group();

        this.createBody();

        this.createHead();

        this.createArms();

        this.createLegs();

        console.log("PlayerModel initialisé.");

    }

    createBody() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            0.8,

            1.2,

            0.4

        );

        const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x3f51b5

        });

        this.body = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.body.position.y = 1.2;

        this.body.castShadow = true;

        this.group.add(this.body);

    }

    createHead() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            0.6,

            0.6,

            0.6

        );

        const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0xffd5b5

        });

        this.head = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.head.position.y = 2.1;

        this.head.castShadow = true;

        this.group.add(this.head);

    }

    createArms() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            0.2,

            0.9,

            0.2

        );

        const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x3f51b5

        });

        this.leftArm = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.rightArm = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.leftArm.position.set(

            -0.55,

            1.25,

            0

        );

        this.rightArm.position.set(

            0.55,

            1.25,

            0

        );

        this.group.add(this.leftArm);

        this.group.add(this.rightArm);

    }

    createLegs() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            0.25,

            1,

            0.25

        );

        const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x222222

        });

        this.leftLeg = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.rightLeg = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.leftLeg.position.set(

            -0.2,

            0.4,

            0

        );

        this.rightLeg.position.set(

            0.2,

            0.4,

            0

        );

        this.group.add(this.leftLeg);

        this.group.add(this.rightLeg);

    }

    getMesh() {

        return this.group;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class PlayerNameplate {

    constructor(game, player) {

        this.game = game;

        this.player = player;

        this.sprite = null;

        this.canvas = null;

        this.context = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        this.canvas.width = 512;
        this.canvas.height = 128;

        this.context = this.canvas.getContext("2d");

        this.updateText(this.player.username);

        const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(this.canvas);

        const material = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({

            map: texture,
            transparent: true

        });

        this.sprite = new THREE.Sprite(material);

        this.sprite.scale.set(

            3,
            0.75,
            1

        );

        if (this.player.mesh) {

            this.player.mesh.add(this.sprite);

            this.sprite.position.set(

                0,

                3,

                0

            );

        }

        console.log("PlayerNameplate initialisé.");

    }

    updateText(username) {

        if (!this.context) {

            return;

        }

        this.context.clearRect(

            0,
            0,
            this.canvas.width,
            this.canvas.height

        );

        this.context.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)";

        this.context.fillRect(

            0,
            20,
            512,
            88

        );

        this.context.font = "Bold 54px Arial";

        this.context.fillStyle = "#ffffff";

        this.context.textAlign = "center";

        this.context.fillText(

            username,

            256,

            78

        );

        if (this.sprite) {

            this.sprite.material.map.needsUpdate = true;

        }

    }

    setUsername(username) {

        this.player.username = username;

        this.updateText(username);

    }

    update(camera) {

        if (!this.sprite || !camera) {

            return;

        }

        this.sprite.quaternion.copy(

            camera.quaternion

        );

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class RemotePlayer {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id = data.id ?? crypto.randomUUID();

        this.username = data.username ?? "RemotePlayer";

        this.skin = data.skin ?? "default";

        this.position = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.targetPosition = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.rotation = new THREE.Euler();

        this.targetRotation = new THREE.Euler();

        this.velocity = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.mesh = null;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.interpolationSpeed = 10;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RemotePlayer initialisé.");

    }

    setMesh(mesh) {

        this.mesh = mesh;

    }

    applySnapshot(snapshot) {

        if (!snapshot) {

            return;

        }

        this.targetPosition.set(

            snapshot.position.x,

            snapshot.position.y,

            snapshot.position.z

        );

        this.targetRotation.set(

            snapshot.rotation.x,

            snapshot.rotation.y,

            snapshot.rotation.z

        );

    }

    teleport(position) {

        this.position.copy(position);

        this.targetPosition.copy(position);

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(position);

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        const alpha = Math.min(

            1,

            deltaTime * this.interpolationSpeed

        );

        this.position.lerp(

            this.targetPosition,

            alpha

        );

        this.rotation.x +=

            (this.targetRotation.x - this.rotation.x) * alpha;

        this.rotation.y +=

            (this.targetRotation.y - this.rotation.y) * alpha;

        this.rotation.z +=

            (this.targetRotation.z - this.rotation.z) * alpha;

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(

                this.position

            );

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(

                this.rotation

            );

        }

    }

}

export class SkinManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.availableSkins = new Map();

        this.loadedSkins = new Map();

        this.defaultSkin = "default";

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("SkinManager initialisé.");

    }

    registerSkin(id, skinData) {

        this.availableSkins.set(

            id,

            skinData

        );

    }

    hasSkin(id) {

        return this.availableSkins.has(id);

    }

    getSkin(id) {

        return this.availableSkins.get(id);

    }

    applySkin(playerModel, skinId) {

        if (!playerModel) {

            return false;

        }

        if (!this.availableSkins.has(skinId)) {

            skinId = this.defaultSkin;

        }

        const skin = this.availableSkins.get(skinId);

        if (!skin) {

            return false;

        }

        if (typeof skin.apply === "function") {

            skin.apply(playerModel);

        }

        playerModel.skinId = skinId;

        return true;

    }

    removeSkin(playerModel) {

        if (!playerModel) {

            return;

        }

        playerModel.skinId = null;

    }

    preloadSkin(id, asset) {

        this.loadedSkins.set(

            id,

            asset

        );

    }

    getLoadedSkin(id) {

        return this.loadedSkins.get(id);

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Future :
        // téléchargement des skins
        // cache
        // skins premium
        // skins saisonniers
        // synchronisation réseau

    }

}

import { InteractiveObject } from "./InteractiveObject.js";

export class Collectible extends InteractiveObject {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        super(game, data);

        this.reward = data.reward ?? null;

        this.value = data.value ?? 0;

        this.collected = false;

        this.rotationSpeed = 1.5;

        this.floatAmplitude = 0.15;

        this.floatSpeed = 2;

        this.baseY = this.position.y;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        await super.initialize();

        console.log("Collectible initialisé.");

    }

    collect(player) {

        if (this.collected) {

            return false;

        }

        this.collected = true;

        this.disableInteraction();

        if (this.game.lootController?.collectReward) {

            this.game.lootController.collectReward(

                player,

                this.reward,

                this.value

            );

        }

        if (this.game.audioManager?.play) {

            this.game.audioManager.play("collect");

        }

        if (this.game.scene && this.mesh) {

            this.game.scene.remove(this.mesh);

        }

        return true;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        super.update(deltaTime);

        if (this.collected || !this.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        this.mesh.rotation.y +=

            this.rotationSpeed * deltaTime;

        this.mesh.position.y =

            this.baseY +

            Math.sin(

                this.elapsedTime * this.floatSpeed

            ) * this.floatAmplitude;

    }

}

import { WorldObject } from "./WorldObject.js";

export class InteractiveObject extends WorldObject {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        super(game, data);

        this.interactionDistance = data.interactionDistance ?? 3;

        this.canInteract = true;

        this.highlighted = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        await super.initialize();

        console.log("InteractiveObject initialisé.");

    }

    canPlayerInteract(playerPosition) {

        if (!this.canInteract) {

            return false;

        }

        const distance = playerPosition.distanceTo(

            this.position

        );

        return distance <= this.interactionDistance;

    }

    interact(player) {

        if (!this.canInteract) {

            return false;

        }

        console.log(

            `${player.username} interagit avec ${this.id}`

        );

        return true;

    }

    setHighlighted(state) {

        this.highlighted = state;

        if (!this.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.mesh.traverse((child) => {

            if (!child.material) {

                return;

            }

            child.material.emissive?.setHex(

                state ? 0x333333 : 0x000000

            );

        });

    }

    enableInteraction() {

        this.canInteract = true;

    }

    disableInteraction() {

        this.canInteract = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        super.update(deltaTime);

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        // Réservé aux futures animations
        // et effets d'interaction.

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class WorldObject {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id = data.id ?? crypto.randomUUID();

        this.type = data.type ?? "object";

        this.position = data.position?.clone() ?? new THREE.Vector3();

        this.rotation = data.rotation?.clone() ?? new THREE.Euler();

        this.scale = data.scale?.clone() ?? new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1);

        this.mesh = null;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.visible = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("WorldObject initialisé.");

    }

    setMesh(mesh) {

        this.mesh = mesh;

        this.syncTransform();

    }

    syncTransform() {

        if (!this.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.mesh.position.copy(this.position);

        this.mesh.rotation.copy(this.rotation);

        this.mesh.scale.copy(this.scale);

    }

    setVisible(value) {

        this.visible = value;

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.visible = value;

        }

    }

    setPosition(position) {

        this.position.copy(position);

        this.syncTransform();

    }

    setRotation(rotation) {

        this.rotation.copy(rotation);

        this.syncTransform();

    }

    setScale(scale) {

        this.scale.copy(scale);

        this.syncTransform();

    }

    destroy() {

        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.mesh && this.game.scene) {

            this.game.scene.remove(this.mesh);

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.syncTransform();

    }

}
