import * as THREE from "three";


export class InteractionController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.raycaster =
            new THREE.Raycaster();

        this.maxDistance = 10;

        this.cooldown = 0.25;

        this.cooldownTimer = 0;

        /*
         * Bloc actuellement tenu en main.
         *
         * null = main vide.
         *
         * Exemples :
         * "grass"
         * "dirt"
         * "stone"
         */
        this.heldBlock = null;


/*
 * Inventaire de blocs disponible
 * pour le prototype de construction.
 *
 * Chaque entrée représente un type
 * de bloc que le joueur peut tenir.
 */
this.blockInventory = [
    {
        id: "grass",
        amount: 64
    },
    {
        id: "dirt",
        amount: 64
    },
    {
        id: "stone",
        amount: 64
    }
];

this.selectedBlockIndex = -1;

this.heldBlockMesh = null;

this.blockColors = {
    grass: 0x6ea84a,
    dirt: 0x8b5a2b,
    stone: 0x808080
};

    }


    async initialize() {

    this.createBlockSelectionUI();

    this.installBlockSelectionInput();

    this.installBlockActionInput();

    console.log(
        "InteractionController initialisé."
    );

}

installBlockSelectionInput() {

    window.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        event => {

            switch (event.code) {

                case "Digit1":

                    this.selectBlock(0);

                    break;


                case "Digit2":

                    this.selectBlock(1);

                    break;


                case "Digit3":

                    this.selectBlock(2);

                    break;


                case "Digit0":

                    this.clearHeldBlock();

                    break;

            }

        }
    );

}
installBlockActionInput() {

    window.addEventListener(
        "pointerdown",
        event => {

            if (!this.enabled) {
                return;
            }

            /*
             * Ne rien faire lorsque la hotbar
             * est touchée.
             */

            if (
                event.target?.closest?.(
                    "#islamcraft-block-hotbar"
                )
            ) {
                return;
            }

            /*
             * Pour une action de bloc,
             * on utilise LA POSITION RÉELLE
             * du doigt / pointeur.
             *
             * Il n'y a aucune distinction
             * clic gauche / clic droit.
             */

            const success =
                this.useBlockAction(
                    event.clientX,
                    event.clientY
                );

            console.log(
                "[BLOCK] Action pointeur :",
                {
                    x: event.clientX,
                    y: event.clientY,
                    success
                }
            );

        }
    );

}

selectBlock(index) {

    if (
        index < 0 ||
        index >= this.blockInventory.length
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const slot =
        this.blockInventory[index];


    if (
        !slot ||
        slot.amount <= 0
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    this.selectedBlockIndex =
        index;

    this.heldBlock =
        slot.id;


    this.updateHeldBlockVisual();

    this.updateBlockSelectionUI();


    console.log(
        "Bloc sélectionné :",
        this.heldBlock
    );


    return true;

}
    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }


        if (this.cooldownTimer > 0) {

            this.cooldownTimer -=
                deltaTime;

        }


        const inputState =
            this.game.inputState;

        if (!inputState) {

            return;

        }


        /*
         * UNE SEULE ACTION :
         *
         * main vide  -> casser
         * bloc tenu  -> poser
         */

        if (
    inputState.interact &&
    this.cooldownTimer <= 0
) {

    console.log(
        "[BLOCK] Action détectée via inputState.interact"
    );

    const success =
        this.useBlockAction();

    console.log(
        "[BLOCK] Résultat de l'action :",
        success
    );

    if (success) {

        this.cooldownTimer =
            this.cooldown;

    }

    inputState.interact = false;

}

    }


    useBlockAction(
    screenX = null,
    screenY = null
) {

    const target =
        this.getTargetBlock(
            screenX,
            screenY
        );

    if (!target) {

        console.log(
            "[BLOCK] Aucun bloc détecté à cette position."
        );

        return false;

    }

    /*
     * MAIN VIDE
     * =========
     *
     * Toucher un bloc sans bloc tenu
     * = casser.
     */

    if (!this.heldBlock) {

        return this.breakBlock(
            target
        );

    }

    /*
     * BLOC EN MAIN
     * =============
     *
     * Toucher un bloc avec un bloc tenu
     * = poser au-dessus de la colonne.
     */

    return this.placeBlock(
        target
    );

}


    getTargetBlock(
    screenX = null,
    screenY = null
) {

    const camera =
        this.game.scene?.getCamera?.();

    if (!camera) {

        console.warn(
            "[BLOCK] Caméra introuvable."
        );

        return null;

    }


    const chunkManager =
        this.game.world
            ?.getChunkManager?.();

    if (!chunkManager) {

        console.warn(
            "[BLOCK] ChunkManager introuvable."
        );

        return null;

    }


    const chunks =
        chunkManager.getLoadedChunks?.();

    if (!chunks) {

        console.warn(
            "[BLOCK] Aucun chunk chargé."
        );

        return null;

    }


    const chunkList =
        chunks instanceof Map
            ? [...chunks.values()]
            : chunks;


    if (
        chunkList.length === 0
    ) {

        console.warn(
            "[BLOCK] Liste de chunks vide."
        );

        return null;

    }


    /*
     * ==================================================
     * CONSTRUCTION DU RAYON
     * ==================================================
     *
     * Si screenX/screenY existent :
     *     rayon depuis l'endroit réellement touché.
     *
     * Sinon :
     *     rayon depuis le centre de l'écran.
     *
     * Le centre est conservé pour les actions
     * clavier comme E.
     */

    let origin =
        camera.getWorldPosition(
            new THREE.Vector3()
        );

    let direction;


    if (
        Number.isFinite(screenX) &&
        Number.isFinite(screenY)
    ) {

        /*
         * Chercher l'élément canvas WebGL.
         */

        const canvas =
            camera
                .parent
                ?.userData
                ?.renderer
                ?.domElement ??
            document.querySelector(
                "canvas"
            );


        if (canvas) {

            const rect =
                canvas.getBoundingClientRect();


            /*
             * Coordonnées normalisées
             * de Three.js :
             *
             * gauche = -1
             * droite = +1
             * haut = +1
             * bas = -1
             */

            const ndcX =
                (
                    (screenX - rect.left) /
                    rect.width
                ) * 2 - 1;


            const ndcY =
                -(
                    (screenY - rect.top) /
                    rect.height
                ) * 2 + 1;


            this.raycaster.setFromCamera(
                new THREE.Vector2(
                    ndcX,
                    ndcY
                ),
                camera
            );


            origin =
                this.raycaster.ray.origin.clone();


            direction =
                this.raycaster.ray.direction
                    .clone()
                    .normalize();

        }
        else {

            console.warn(
                "[BLOCK] Canvas WebGL introuvable."
            );

            direction =
                camera.getWorldDirection(
                    new THREE.Vector3()
                ).normalize();

        }

    }
    else {

        direction =
            camera.getWorldDirection(
                new THREE.Vector3()
            ).normalize();

    }


    /*
     * ==================================================
     * RAYCAST VOXEL
     * ==================================================
     *
     * On ne dépend PAS du raycast Three.js
     * sur les InstancedMesh.
     *
     * On teste directement les colonnes utilisées
     * par ChunkRenderer.
     */

    const step =
        0.025;


    for (
        let distance = 0;
        distance <= this.maxDistance;
        distance += step
    ) {

        const point =
            origin
                .clone()
                .add(
                    direction
                        .clone()
                        .multiplyScalar(
                            distance
                        )
                );


        /*
         * Tester le chunk correspondant
         * à la position du rayon.
         */

        for (
            const chunk of chunkList
        ) {

            if (
                !chunk ||
                !Array.isArray(
                    chunk.terrain
                )
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            const size =
                chunk.size;


            const worldMinX =
                chunk.chunkX * size;


            const worldMaxX =
                worldMinX + size;


            const worldMinZ =
                chunk.chunkZ * size;


            const worldMaxZ =
                worldMinZ + size;


            if (
                point.x < worldMinX ||
                point.x >= worldMaxX ||
                point.z < worldMinZ ||
                point.z >= worldMaxZ
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            const localX =
                Math.floor(
                    point.x - worldMinX
                );


            const localZ =
                Math.floor(
                    point.z - worldMinZ
                );


            if (
                localX < 0 ||
                localX >= size ||
                localZ < 0 ||
                localZ >= size
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            const rawHeight =
                Number(
                    chunk
                        .terrain[
                            localZ
                        ]?.[
                            localX
                        ]
                );


            if (
                !Number.isFinite(
                    rawHeight
                )
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            /*
             * ChunkRenderer utilise exactement
             * cette formule :
             *
             * height =
             * max(
             *     1,
             *     floor(rawHeight) + 1
             * )
             */

            const height =
                Math.max(
                    1,
                    Math.floor(
                        rawHeight
                    ) + 1
                );


            /*
             * Le rayon doit être dans la partie
             * verticale réellement occupée
             * par la colonne.
             */

            if (
                point.y < 0 ||
                point.y >= height
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            const localY =
                Math.floor(
                    point.y
                );


            /*
             * Vérifier qu'il ne s'agit pas
             * d'un bloc déjà supprimé.
             */

            if (
                chunk.isBlockRemoved?.(
                    localX,
                    localY,
                    localZ
                )
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            console.log(
                "[BLOCK] ✅ BLOC DÉTECTÉ",
                {
                    chunk:
                        chunk.key,

                    localX,

                    localY,

                    localZ,

                    distance,

                    point:
                        point.toArray()
                }
            );


            /*
             * Déterminer la face touchée
             * à partir de la direction du rayon.
             *
             * Pour le système actuel, la pose
             * est volontairement limitée au-dessus
             * de la colonne.
             */

            const faceNormal =
                new THREE.Vector3(
                    0,
                    1,
                    0
                );


            return {

                chunk,

                chunkKey:
                    chunk.key,

                localX,

                localY,

                localZ,

                faceNormal,

                point:
                    point.clone(),

                distance

            };

        }

    }


    console.log(
        "[BLOCK] Aucun bloc détecté.",
        {
            origin:
                origin.toArray(),

            direction:
                direction.toArray(),

            maxDistance:
                this.maxDistance
        }
    );


    return null;

}


    breakBlock(target) {

    if (!target) {

        return false;

    }


    const chunk =
        target.chunk;


    if (!chunk) {

        return false;

    }


    const x =
        target.localX;

    const y =
        target.localY;

    const z =
        target.localZ;


    if (
        !chunk.isBlockRemoved ||
        !chunk.removeBlock
    ) {

        console.error(
            "[BLOCK] Le Chunk ne possède pas le système removeBlock()."
        );

        return false;

    }


    /*
     * Ne jamais casser le dernier bloc
     * sous la colonne.
     */

    const rawHeight =
        Number(
            chunk.terrain[z]?.[x]
        );


    if (
        !Number.isFinite(
            rawHeight
        )
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const height =
        Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(
                rawHeight
            ) + 1
        );


    const topY =
        height - 1;


    /*
     * Pour le système actuel basé sur
     * des colonnes, on casse uniquement
     * le bloc réellement situé au sommet.
     */

    if (
        y !== topY
    ) {

        console.warn(
            "[BLOCK] Le bloc détecté n'est pas le sommet de la colonne.",
            {
                y,
                topY
            }
        );

        return false;

    }


    /*
     * Vérifier qu'il n'est pas déjà supprimé.
     */

    if (
        chunk.isBlockRemoved(
            x,
            y,
            z
        )
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    /*
     * UTILISER LE SYSTÈME NATIF DU CHUNK.
     */

    const removed =
        chunk.removeBlock(
            x,
            y,
            z
        );


    if (!removed) {

        console.warn(
            "[BLOCK] removeBlock() a échoué."
        );

        return false;

    }


    /*
     * Donner le bloc cassé au joueur.
     */

    const inventorySlot =
        this.blockInventory.find(
            slot =>
                slot.id === "grass"
        );


    if (inventorySlot) {

        inventorySlot.amount += 1;

    }


    /*
     * Reconstruire le rendu.
     */

    this.rebuildChunk(
        chunk
    );


    console.log(
        "[BLOCK] ✅ BLOC CASSÉ",
        {
            chunk:
                target.chunkKey,

            x,

            y,

            z
        }
    );


    return true;

}


    placeBlock(target) {

        if (!this.heldBlock) {

            return false;

        }


        if (
            !target.faceNormal
        ) {

            return false;

        }


        const normal =
            target.faceNormal;


        /*
         * Le nouveau bloc est placé
         * juste à côté du bloc touché,
         * sur la face touchée.
         */

        const targetWorldX =
            target.chunk.chunkX *
            target.chunk.size +
            target.localX;

        const targetWorldY =
            target.localY;

        const targetWorldZ =
            target.chunk.chunkZ *
            target.chunk.size +
            target.localZ;


        const worldX =
            targetWorldX +
            Math.round(normal.x);

        const worldY =
            targetWorldY +
            Math.round(normal.y);

        const worldZ =
            targetWorldZ +
            Math.round(normal.z);


        const chunkSize =
            target.chunk.size;


        const chunkX =
            Math.floor(
                worldX /
                chunkSize
            );

        const chunkZ =
            Math.floor(
                worldZ /
                chunkSize
            );


        const localX =
            worldX -
            chunkX *
            chunkSize;

        const localZ =
            worldZ -
            chunkZ *
            chunkSize;


        const chunkManager =
            this.game.world
                ?.getChunkManager?.();


        if (!chunkManager) {

            return false;

        }


        const targetChunk =
    chunkManager.getChunk(
        chunkX,
        chunkZ
    ) ??
    chunkManager.chunks?.get?.(
        `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`
    );


if (!targetChunk) {

    console.warn(
        "Impossible de trouver le chunk pour poser :",
        `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`
    );

    return false;

}


        /*
         * Vérifier que la position est
         * disponible.
         */

        if (
            !Array.isArray(
                targetChunk.terrain
            )
        ) {

            return false;

        }


        /*
         * Avec le terrain actuel, le système
         * de colonnes ne permet pas encore
         * de représenter un bloc placé
         * au milieu d'une colonne.
         *
         * On autorise donc pour l'instant
         * uniquement une pose au-dessus
         * de la colonne.
         */

        const columnHeight =
            Number(
                targetChunk
                    .terrain[localZ]
                    ?.[localX]
            );


        if (
            !Number.isFinite(
                columnHeight
            )
        ) {

            return false;

        }


        if (
            worldY !==
            Math.floor(
                columnHeight
            ) + 1
        ) {

            return false;

        }


        targetChunk.terrain[localZ][localX] =
            worldY;

            if (
    this.selectedBlockIndex >= 0
) {

    const slot =
        this.blockInventory[
            this.selectedBlockIndex
        ];


    slot.amount =
        Math.max(
            0,
            slot.amount - 1
        );


    if (
        slot.amount <= 0
    ) {

        this.clearHeldBlock();

    }

}


        this.rebuildChunk(
            targetChunk
        );


        console.log(
            "Bloc posé :",
            this.heldBlock,
            worldX,
            worldY,
            worldZ
        );


        return true;

    }


rebuildChunk(chunk) {

    const renderer =
        this.game.world
            ?.getChunkManager?.()
            ?.loader
            ?.renderer;


    if (!renderer) {

        console.error(
            "[BLOCK] ChunkRenderer introuvable."
        );

        return false;

    }


    if (
        typeof renderer.rebuild !==
        "function"
    ) {

        console.error(
            "[BLOCK] ChunkRenderer.rebuild() introuvable."
        );

        return false;

    }


    renderer.rebuild(
        chunk
    );


    console.log(
        "[BLOCK] Rendu du chunk reconstruit :",
        chunk.key
    );


    return true;

}


    setHeldBlock(block) {

    if (
        block === null ||
        block === undefined
    ) {

        this.clearHeldBlock();

        return;

    }


    const index =
        this.blockInventory.findIndex(
            slot =>
                slot.id === String(block) &&
                slot.amount > 0
        );


    if (index === -1) {

        return false;

    }


    this.selectedBlockIndex =
        index;

    this.heldBlock =
        this.blockInventory[index].id;


    this.updateHeldBlockVisual();

    this.updateBlockSelectionUI();


    return true;

}


   clearHeldBlock() {

    this.heldBlock = null;

    this.selectedBlockIndex = -1;

    this.removeHeldBlockVisual();

    this.updateBlockSelectionUI();

}

    updateHeldBlockVisual() {

    const player =
        this.game.localPlayer;

    const rightArm =
        player
            ?.model
            ?.rightArm;


    if (!rightArm) {

        return;

    }


    this.removeHeldBlockVisual();


    if (!this.heldBlock) {

        return;

    }


    const geometry =
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(
            0.38,
            0.38,
            0.38
        );


    const material =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color:
                this.blockColors[
                    this.heldBlock
                ] ??
                0xffffff

        });


    const blockMesh =
        new THREE.Mesh(
            geometry,
            material
        );


    /*
     * Le bloc est placé devant
     * la main droite.
     */

    blockMesh.position.set(
        0,
        -0.48,
        0.18
    );


    blockMesh.rotation.set(
        0,
        0,
        0
    );


    blockMesh.castShadow = true;

    blockMesh.receiveShadow = true;


    blockMesh.userData.isHeldBlock =
        true;


    rightArm.add(
        blockMesh
    );


    this.heldBlockMesh =
        blockMesh;

}

removeHeldBlockVisual() {

    if (
        !this.heldBlockMesh
    ) {

        return;

    }


    const mesh =
        this.heldBlockMesh;


    mesh.parent?.remove(
        mesh
    );


    mesh.geometry?.dispose();


    if (mesh.material) {

        mesh.material.dispose();

    }


    this.heldBlockMesh = null;

}

createBlockSelectionUI() {

    if (
        document.getElementById(
            "islamcraft-block-hotbar"
        )
    ) {

        return;

    }


    const hotbar =
        document.createElement(
            "div"
        );


    hotbar.id =
        "islamcraft-block-hotbar";


    hotbar.style.position =
        "fixed";

    hotbar.style.left =
        "50%";

    hotbar.style.bottom =
        "20px";

    hotbar.style.transform =
        "translateX(-50%)";

    hotbar.style.display =
        "flex";

    hotbar.style.gap =
        "6px";

    hotbar.style.zIndex =
        "1000";


    const names = [
        "grass",
        "dirt",
        "stone"
    ];


    names.forEach(
        (name, index) => {

            const slot =
                document.createElement(
                    "div"
                );


            slot.dataset.index =
                String(index);


            slot.style.width =
                "52px";

            slot.style.height =
                "52px";

            slot.style.border =
                "3px solid white";

            slot.style.background =
                "rgba(0,0,0,0.65)";

            slot.style.color =
                "white";

            slot.style.display =
                "flex";

            slot.style.alignItems =
                "center";

            slot.style.justifyContent =
                "center";

            slot.style.fontFamily =
                "Arial";

            slot.style.fontWeight =
                "bold";

            slot.style.userSelect =
                "none";


            slot.textContent =
                `${index + 1}`;


            slot.title =
                name;


            slot.addEventListener(
                "click",
                () => {

                    this.selectBlock(
                        index
                    );

                }
            );


            hotbar.appendChild(
                slot
            );

        }
    );


    const emptySlot =
        document.createElement(
            "div"
        );


    emptySlot.dataset.index =
        "-1";


    emptySlot.style.width =
        "52px";

    emptySlot.style.height =
        "52px";

    emptySlot.style.border =
        "3px solid white";

    emptySlot.style.background =
        "rgba(0,0,0,0.65)";

    emptySlot.style.color =
        "white";

    emptySlot.style.display =
        "flex";

    emptySlot.style.alignItems =
        "center";

    emptySlot.style.justifyContent =
        "center";

    emptySlot.textContent =
        "0";


    emptySlot.addEventListener(
        "click",
        () => {

            this.clearHeldBlock();

        }
    );


    hotbar.appendChild(
        emptySlot
    );


    document.body.appendChild(
        hotbar
    );


    this.updateBlockSelectionUI();

}

updateBlockSelectionUI() {

    const hotbar =
        document.getElementById(
            "islamcraft-block-hotbar"
        );


    if (!hotbar) {

        return;

    }


    const slots =
        hotbar.children;


    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < slots.length;
        i++
    ) {

        const selected =
            this.selectedBlockIndex === i;


        slots[i].style.outline =
            selected
                ? "4px solid yellow"
                : "none";

    }


    /*
     * Le dernier slot correspond
     * à la main vide.
     */

    if (
        slots.length > 3
    ) {

        slots[3].style.outline =
            this.selectedBlockIndex === -1
                ? "4px solid yellow"
                : "none";

    }

}


    getHeldBlock() {

        return this.heldBlock;

    }


    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled =
            Boolean(enabled);

    }


    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

export class InteractionDetector {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.interactionRadius = 3;

        this.interactiveObjects = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("InteractionDetector initialisé.");

    }

    register(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return;

        }

        if (!this.interactiveObjects.includes(object)) {

            this.interactiveObjects.push(object);

        }

    }

    unregister(object) {

        this.interactiveObjects = this.interactiveObjects.filter(

            entry => entry !== object

        );

    }

    findNearest(playerPosition) {

        let nearestObject = null;

        let nearestDistance = Infinity;

        for (const object of this.interactiveObjects) {

            if (!object.enabled || !object.canInteract) {

                continue;

            }

            const distance = playerPosition.distanceTo(

                object.position

            );

            if (

                distance <= this.interactionRadius &&

                distance < nearestDistance

            ) {

                nearestDistance = distance;

                nearestObject = object;

            }

        }

        return nearestObject;

    }

    clear() {

        this.interactiveObjects.length = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé aux futures optimisations :
        // - Spatial Hash
        // - QuadTree
        // - Chunk Culling
        // - Détection par secteur
        // - Priorité des interactions

    }

}

export class InteractionPrompt {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.target = null;

        this.visible = false;

        this.element = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.element = document.createElement("div");

        this.element.id = "interactionPrompt";

        this.element.style.position = "absolute";
        this.element.style.bottom = "12%";
        this.element.style.left = "50%";
        this.element.style.transform = "translateX(-50%)";

        this.element.style.padding = "12px 20px";

        this.element.style.borderRadius = "10px";

        this.element.style.background = "rgba(0,0,0,0.75)";

        this.element.style.color = "white";

        this.element.style.fontFamily = "Arial";

        this.element.style.fontSize = "18px";

        this.element.style.display = "none";

        this.element.style.pointerEvents = "none";

        this.element.style.zIndex = "1000";

        document.body.appendChild(this.element);

        console.log("InteractionPrompt initialisé.");

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

        if (!target) {

            this.hide();

            return;

        }

        this.show(

            target.displayName ||

            "Interagir"

        );

    }

    show(label) {

        this.visible = true;

        this.element.style.display = "block";

        const mobile =

            "ontouchstart" in window;

        const key = mobile ? "👆" : "[E]";

        this.element.innerHTML =

            `${key} ${label}`;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

        this.target = null;

        this.element.style.display = "none";

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        // Futures animations :
        // Fade In
        // Fade Out
        // Pulse
        // Icônes

    }

}

export class LootController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.pendingRewards = [];

        this.autoCollect = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LootController initialisé.");

    }

    generateLoot(source) {

        if (!source) {

            return [];

        }

        const rewards =

            this.game.lootTable?.generate(

                source

            ) ?? [];

        this.pendingRewards.push(

            ...rewards

        );

        this.game.rewardAnimation?.play(

            rewards,

            source.position

        );

        this.game.lootPresentation?.show(

            rewards

        );

        return rewards;

    }

    collectReward(player, reward, amount = 1) {

        if (!reward) {

            return;

        }

        switch (reward.type) {

            case "xp":

                this.game.playerProfile?.addExperience(

                    amount

                );

                break;

            case "coins":

                this.game.playerInventory?.addCoins(

                    amount

                );

                break;

            case "item":

                this.game.playerInventory?.addItem(

                    reward.id,

                    amount

                );

                break;

            default:

                console.warn(

                    "Récompense inconnue :", reward

                );

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:collected",

            {

                reward,

                amount

            }

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.pendingRewards.length = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // AutoCollect
        // Loot magnétique
        // Expiration
        // Synchronisation réseau

    }

}

export class LootPresentation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.queue = [];

        this.displayDuration = 3;

        this.currentDisplay = null;

        this.displayTimer = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LootPresentation initialisé.");

    }

    show(rewards) {

        if (!Array.isArray(rewards) || rewards.length === 0) {

            return;

        }

        this.queue.push(...rewards);

    }

    display(reward) {

        this.currentDisplay = reward;

        this.displayTimer = this.displayDuration;

        this.game.uiManager?.showLootReward?.(

            reward

        );

    }

    hide() {

        this.currentDisplay = null;

        this.game.uiManager?.hideLootReward?.();

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.currentDisplay && this.queue.length > 0) {

            this.display(

                this.queue.shift()

            );

        }

        if (!this.currentDisplay) {

            return;

        }

        this.displayTimer -= deltaTime;

        if (this.displayTimer <= 0) {

            this.hide();

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.queue.length = 0;

        this.hide();

    }

}

================================================== 
export class RewardAnimation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.activeAnimations = [];

        this.defaultDuration = 1.5;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RewardAnimation initialisé.");

    }

    play(rewards, position = null) {

        if (!Array.isArray(rewards)) {

            rewards = [rewards];

        }

        for (const reward of rewards) {

            this.activeAnimations.push({

                reward,

                position,

                elapsed: 0,

                duration: this.defaultDuration

            });

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (let i = this.activeAnimations.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {

            const animation = this.activeAnimations[i];

            animation.elapsed += deltaTime;

            const progress =

                animation.elapsed /

                animation.duration;

            this.game.particleManager?.spawnRewardEffect?.(

                animation.reward,

                animation.position,

                progress

            );

            if (progress >= 1) {

                this.activeAnimations.splice(i, 1);

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.activeAnimations.length = 0;

    }

    isPlaying() {

        return this.activeAnimations.length > 0;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class CollisionController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        /*
         * Taille horizontale approximative
         * du joueur.
         */

        this.playerRadius = 0.25;


        /*
         * Hauteur du joueur.
         *
         * L'origine du modèle est proche
         * du niveau des pieds.
         */

        this.playerHeight = 2.5;


        /*
         * Hauteur maximale qu'un joueur
         * peut monter automatiquement.
         *
         * Un bloc = 1 unité.
         */

        this.maxStepHeight = 1.0;


        /*
         * Gravité.
         */

        this.gravity = 24;


        /*
         * Vitesse verticale.
         */

        this.verticalVelocity = 0;


        /*
         * Tolérance pour rester posé
         * proprement sur le bloc.
         */

        this.groundTolerance = 0.05;

    }


    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "CollisionController initialisé."
        );

    }


    getChunkManager() {

        return (
            this.game.world?.getChunkManager?.()
            ??
            this.game.worldManager?.getChunkManager?.()
            ??
            this.game.chunkManager
            ??
            null
        );

    }


    getChunkSize() {

        return Number(
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize
            ?? 16
        );

    }


    getChunkAt(x, z) {

        const chunkManager =
            this.getChunkManager();

        if (!chunkManager) {
            return null;
        }


        const chunkSize =
            this.getChunkSize();


        const chunkX =
            Math.floor(
                x / chunkSize
            );

        const chunkZ =
            Math.floor(
                z / chunkSize
            );


        return chunkManager.getChunk(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

    }


    getTerrainHeight(x, z) {

        const chunk =
            this.getChunkAt(
                x,
                z
            );

        if (
            !chunk ||
            !Array.isArray(
                chunk.terrain
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }


        const chunkSize =
            this.getChunkSize();


        const chunkX =
            Math.floor(
                x / chunkSize
            );

        const chunkZ =
            Math.floor(
                z / chunkSize
            );


        let localX =
            Math.floor(
                x -
                chunkX * chunkSize
            );

        let localZ =
            Math.floor(
                z -
                chunkZ * chunkSize
            );


        localX =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    chunkSize - 1,
                    localX
                )
            );

        localZ =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    chunkSize - 1,
                    localZ
                )
            );


        const rawHeight =
            Number(
                chunk.terrain[
                    localZ
                ]?.[
                    localX
                ]
            );


        if (
            !Number.isFinite(
                rawHeight
            )
        ) {
            return null;
        }


        /*
         * IMPORTANT :
         *
         * ChunkRenderer transforme actuellement
         * rawHeight en :
         *
         * Math.floor(rawHeight) + 1
         *
         * donc la hauteur de collision doit
         * utiliser exactement la même conversion.
         */

        return Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(
                rawHeight
            ) + 1
        );

    }


    getGroundHeightAround(x, z) {

        const r =
            this.playerRadius;


        const points = [

            [x, z],

            [x - r, z],

            [x + r, z],

            [x, z - r],

            [x, z + r]

        ];


        let highestGround = null;


        for (
            const [px, pz] of points
        ) {

            const height =
                this.getTerrainHeight(
                    px,
                    pz
                );


            /*
             * Chunk pas encore chargé.
             */

            if (
                height === null
            ) {
                return null;
            }


            if (
                highestGround === null ||
                height > highestGround
            ) {

                highestGround =
                    height;

            }

        }


        return highestGround;

    }


    canStepTo(
        currentPosition,
        targetX,
        targetZ
    ) {

        const currentGround =
            this.getGroundHeightAround(
                currentPosition.x,
                currentPosition.z
            );

        const targetGround =
            this.getGroundHeightAround(
                targetX,
                targetZ
            );


        /*
         * Impossible de calculer :
         * le chunk n'est pas encore chargé.
         */

        if (
            currentGround === null ||
            targetGround === null
        ) {

            return {
                allowed: false,
                unknown: true,
                ground: null
            };

        }


        const difference =
            targetGround -
            currentGround;


        /*
         * Terrain qui monte d'au plus
         * un bloc :
         *
         * le joueur peut monter.
         */

        if (
            difference > 0 &&
            difference <=
            this.maxStepHeight
        ) {

            return {
                allowed: true,
                ground: targetGround,
                stepUp: difference
            };

        }


        /*
         * Terrain qui monte de plus
         * d'un bloc :
         *
         * impossible.
         */

        if (
            difference >
            this.maxStepHeight
        ) {

            return {
                allowed: false,
                unknown: false,
                ground: targetGround,
                stepUp: difference
            };

        }


        /*
         * Terrain plat ou descendant :
         * autorisé.
         */

        return {
            allowed: true,
            ground: targetGround,
            stepUp: 0
        };

    }


    resolveMovement(
        currentPosition,
        movement
    ) {

        if (
            !currentPosition ||
            !movement
        ) {

            return new THREE.Vector3();

        }


        const result =
            new THREE.Vector3();


        /*
         * =========================
         * AXE X
         * =========================
         */

        if (
            Math.abs(movement.x) >
            0.000001
        ) {

            const targetX =
                currentPosition.x +
                movement.x;


            const collision =
                this.canStepTo(
                    currentPosition,
                    targetX,
                    currentPosition.z
                );


            if (
                collision.allowed
            ) {

                result.x =
                    movement.x;

            }

        }


        /*
         * =========================
         * AXE Z
         * =========================
         */

        if (
            Math.abs(movement.z) >
            0.000001
        ) {

            const intermediateX =
                currentPosition.x +
                result.x;


            const targetZ =
                currentPosition.z +
                movement.z;


            const collision =
                this.canStepTo(
                    currentPosition,
                    intermediateX,
                    targetZ
                );


            if (
                collision.allowed
            ) {

                result.z =
                    movement.z;

            }

        }


        /*
         * =========================
         * AJUSTEMENT DE HAUTEUR
         * =========================
         */

        const finalX =
            currentPosition.x +
            result.x;

        const finalZ =
            currentPosition.z +
            result.z;


        const finalGround =
            this.getGroundHeightAround(
                finalX,
                finalZ
            );


        if (
            finalGround !== null
        ) {

            const currentGround =
                this.getGroundHeightAround(
                    currentPosition.x,
                    currentPosition.z
                );


            if (
                currentGround !== null
            ) {

                const difference =
                    finalGround -
                    currentGround;


                /*
                 * Monter un seul bloc :
                 * on remonte le joueur.
                 */

                if (
                    difference > 0 &&
                    difference <=
                    this.maxStepHeight
                ) {

                    result.y +=
                        difference;

                }

            }

        }


        return result;

    }


    applyGravity(
        deltaTime
    ) {

        const player =
            this.game.localPlayer;

        if (!player) {
            return;
        }


        const ground =
            this.getGroundHeightAround(
                player.position.x,
                player.position.z
            );


        /*
         * Chunk inconnu :
         * ne pas faire tomber le joueur
         * dans une zone pas encore chargée.
         */

        if (
            ground === null
        ) {
            return;
        }


        /*
         * Le joueur est au-dessus du sol.
         */

        if (
            player.position.y >
            ground +
            this.groundTolerance
        ) {

            this.verticalVelocity -=
                this.gravity *
                deltaTime;


            let nextY =
                player.position.y +
                this.verticalVelocity *
                deltaTime;


            /*
             * Le joueur touche le sol.
             */

            if (
                nextY <= ground
            ) {

                nextY =
                    ground;

                this.verticalVelocity =
                    0;

            }


            player.setPosition(
                player.position.x,
                nextY,
                player.position.z
            );

        }

        else {

            /*
             * Maintenir exactement
             * le joueur sur le bloc.
             */

            player.setPosition(
                player.position.x,
                ground,
                player.position.z
            );


            this.verticalVelocity =
                0;

        }

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        this.applyGravity(
            deltaTime
        );

    }


    clear() {

        this.verticalVelocity =
            0;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class GroundDetector {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();

        this.rayLength = 1.2;

        this.isGrounded = false;

        this.groundNormal = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);

        this.groundHeight = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("GroundDetector initialisé.");

    }

    update(player) {

        if (!player || !player.position) {

            return;

        }

        this.raycaster.set(

            player.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.5, 0)),

            new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0)

        );

        const terrain = this.game.worldManager?.terrainMesh;

        if (!terrain) {

            this.isGrounded = false;

            return;

        }

        const hits = this.raycaster.intersectObject(

            terrain,

            true

        );

        if (hits.length === 0) {

            this.isGrounded = false;

            return;

        }

        const hit = hits[0];

        if (hit.distance <= this.rayLength) {

            this.isGrounded = true;

            this.groundHeight = hit.point.y;

            this.groundNormal.copy(hit.face.normal);

        } else {

            this.isGrounded = false;

        }

    }

    getGroundHeight() {

        return this.groundHeight;

    }

    getGroundNormal() {

        return this.groundNormal;

    }

    grounded() {

        return this.isGrounded;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class MovementController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.moveDirection = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.cameraForward = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.cameraRight = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.velocity = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.currentSpeed = 0;

        this.walkSpeed = 6;

        this.runSpeed = 9;

        this.acceleration = 18;

        this.deceleration = 20;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MovementController initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        const player = this.game.localPlayer;

        const camera = this.game.camera;

        const input = this.game.inputManager?.getMovement?.();

        if (!player || !camera || !input) {

            return;

        }

        this.moveDirection.set(0, 0, 0);

        camera.getWorldDirection(this.cameraForward);

        this.cameraForward.y = 0;

        this.cameraForward.normalize();

        this.cameraRight.crossVectors(

            this.cameraForward,

            new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)

        );

        this.cameraRight.normalize();

        this.moveDirection.addScaledVector(

            this.cameraForward,

            input.forward

        );

        this.moveDirection.addScaledVector(

            this.cameraRight,

            input.right

        );

        if (this.moveDirection.lengthSq() > 0) {

            this.moveDirection.normalize();

            this.currentSpeed +=

                this.acceleration * deltaTime;

        } else {

            this.currentSpeed -=

                this.deceleration * deltaTime;

        }

        this.currentSpeed = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(

            this.currentSpeed,

            0,

            this.walkSpeed

        );

        this.velocity.copy(

            this.moveDirection

        ).multiplyScalar(

            this.currentSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        player.position.add(

            this.velocity

        );

        if (this.moveDirection.lengthSq() > 0.0001) {

            const targetRotation = Math.atan2(

                this.moveDirection.x,

                this.moveDirection.z

            );

            player.rotation.y +=

                (targetRotation - player.rotation.y)

                * Math.min(

                    1,

                    deltaTime * 10

                );

        }

        this.game.playerAnimator?.update(

            deltaTime,

            this.velocity

        );

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class MovementSmoothing {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.positionLerpSpeed = 15;

        this.rotationLerpSpeed = 12;

        this.targetPosition = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.targetRotation = new THREE.Euler();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MovementSmoothing initialisé.");

    }

    setTarget(position, rotation) {

        if (position) {

            this.targetPosition.copy(position);

        }

        if (rotation) {

            this.targetRotation.copy(rotation);

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime, object) {

        if (!this.enabled || !object) {

            return;

        }

        object.position.lerp(

            this.targetPosition,

            Math.min(

                1,

                deltaTime * this.positionLerpSpeed

            )

        );

        object.rotation.x +=

            (this.targetRotation.x - object.rotation.x)

            * Math.min(

                1,

                deltaTime * this.rotationLerpSpeed

            );

        object.rotation.y +=

            (this.targetRotation.y - object.rotation.y)

            * Math.min(

                1,

                deltaTime * this.rotationLerpSpeed

            );

        object.rotation.z +=

            (this.targetRotation.z - object.rotation.z)

            * Math.min(

                1,

                deltaTime * this.rotationLerpSpeed

            );

    }

    teleport(object, position, rotation) {

        if (!object) {

            return;

        }

        object.position.copy(position);

        object.rotation.copy(rotation);

        this.targetPosition.copy(position);

        this.targetRotation.copy(rotation);

    }

    reset() {

        this.targetPosition.set(0, 0, 0);

        this.targetRotation.set(0, 0, 0);

    }

}

export const MovementStates = Object.freeze({

    IDLE: "idle",

    WALKING: "walking",

    RUNNING: "running",

    JUMPING: "jumping",

    FALLING: "falling",

    LANDING: "landing",

    SLIDING: "sliding"

});

export class MovementState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentState = MovementStates.IDLE;

        this.previousState = null;

        this.stateTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MovementState initialisé.");

    }

    setState(newState) {

        if (newState === this.currentState) {

            return;

        }

        this.previousState = this.currentState;

        this.currentState = newState;

        this.stateTime = 0;

    }

    is(state) {

        return this.currentState === state;

    }

    update(deltaTime, velocity, isGrounded = true) {

        this.stateTime += deltaTime;

        const speed = velocity.length();

        if (!isGrounded) {

            if (velocity.y > 0) {

                this.setState(

                    MovementStates.JUMPING

                );

            } else {

                this.setState(

                    MovementStates.FALLING

                );

            }

            return;

        }

        if (speed < 0.05) {

            this.setState(

                MovementStates.IDLE

            );

            return;

        }

        if (speed < 5) {

            this.setState(

                MovementStates.WALKING

            );

            return;

        }

        this.setState(

            MovementStates.RUNNING

        );

    }

    reset() {

        this.previousState = null;

        this.currentState = MovementStates.IDLE;

        this.stateTime = 0;

    }

}

export class LevelController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.level = 1;

        this.maxLevel = 100;

        this.baseXP = 100;

        this.growthFactor = 1.25;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LevelController initialisé.");

    }

    getLevel() {

        return this.level;

    }

    setLevel(level) {

        this.level = Math.max(

            1,

            Math.min(level, this.maxLevel)

        );

    }

    canLevelUp() {

        const xpController = this.game.xpController;

        if (!xpController) {

            return false;

        }

        return (

            xpController.getCurrentXP() >=

            this.getRequiredXP()

        );

    }

    levelUp() {

        if (!this.canLevelUp()) {

            return false;

        }

        const xpController = this.game.xpController;

        xpController.consumeXP(

            this.getRequiredXP()

        );

        this.level++;

        this.game.unlockController?.onLevelUp(

            this.level

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "player:level-up",

            {

                level: this.level

            }

        );

        return true;

    }

    getRequiredXP() {

        return Math.floor(

            this.baseXP *

            Math.pow(

                this.growthFactor,

                this.level - 1

            )

        );

    }

    getProgress() {

        const xpController = this.game.xpController;

        if (!xpController) {

            return 0;

        }

        return (

            xpController.getCurrentXP() /

            this.getRequiredXP()

        );

    }

    reset() {

        this.level = 1;

    }

}

export class ProgressionController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("ProgressionController initialisé.");

    }

    addXP(amount) {

        if (!this.enabled || amount <= 0) {

            return false;

        }

        const xpController = this.game.xpController;

        const levelController = this.game.levelController;

        if (!xpController || !levelController) {

            return false;

        }

        xpController.addXP(amount);

        while (levelController.canLevelUp()) {

            levelController.levelUp();

        }

        return true;

    }

    getLevel() {

        return this.game.levelController?.getLevel() ?? 1;

    }

    getCurrentXP() {

        return this.game.xpController?.getCurrentXP() ?? 0;

    }

    getRequiredXP() {

        return this.game.levelController?.getRequiredXP() ?? 0;

    }

    getProgress() {

        return this.game.levelController?.getProgress() ?? 0;

    }

    reset() {

        this.game.xpController?.reset();

        this.game.levelController?.reset();

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // Bonus temporaires
        // Multiplicateurs d'XP
        // Événements saisonniers
        // Synchronisation réseau

    }

}

export class UnlockController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.unlockedFeatures = new Set();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("UnlockController initialisé.");

    }

    onLevelUp(level) {

        const unlocks = this.getUnlocksForLevel(level);

        for (const unlock of unlocks) {

            this.unlock(unlock);

        }

    }

    unlock(featureId) {

        if (this.unlockedFeatures.has(featureId)) {

            return false;

        }

        this.unlockedFeatures.add(featureId);

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "progression:unlock",

            {

                featureId

            }

        );

        return true;

    }

    lock(featureId) {

        this.unlockedFeatures.delete(featureId);

    }

    isUnlocked(featureId) {

        return this.unlockedFeatures.has(featureId);

    }

    getUnlocksForLevel(level) {

        const unlockTable =

            this.game.gameData?.progression?.unlockTable

            ?? {};

        return unlockTable[level] ?? [];

    }

    getUnlockedFeatures() {

        return [

            ...this.unlockedFeatures

        ];

    }

    reset() {

        this.unlockedFeatures.clear();

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Extensions futures :
        // Déblocages temporaires
        // Événements spéciaux
        // Pass saisonnier
        // Succès

    }

}

export class XPController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentXP = 0;

        this.totalXP = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("XPController initialisé.");

    }

    addXP(amount) {

        if (amount <= 0) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentXP += amount;

        this.totalXP += amount;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "xp:added",

            {

                amount,

                currentXP: this.currentXP,

                totalXP: this.totalXP

            }

        );

    }

    consumeXP(amount) {

        this.currentXP = Math.max(

            0,

            this.currentXP - amount

        );

    }

    getCurrentXP() {

        return this.currentXP;

    }

    getTotalXP() {

        return this.totalXP;

    }

    setCurrentXP(amount) {

        this.currentXP = Math.max(

            0,

            amount

        );

    }

    reset() {

        this.currentXP = 0;

        this.totalXP = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Extensions futures :
        // Bonus XP temporaires
        // Multiplicateurs
        // Double XP
        // Synchronisation réseau

    }

}

================================================== 
export class AnswerController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.caseSensitive = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AnswerController initialisé.");

    }

    check(question, answer) {

        if (!question) {

            return false;

        }

        if (Array.isArray(question.correctAnswers)) {

            return this.checkMultiple(

                question,

                answer

            );

        }

        return this.checkSingle(

            question,

            answer

        );

    }

    checkSingle(question, answer) {

        const expected =

            this.normalize(

                question.correctAnswer

            );

        const received =

            this.normalize(

                answer

            );

        return expected === received;

    }

    checkMultiple(question, answers) {

        if (!Array.isArray(answers)) {

            return false;

        }

        const expected =

            question.correctAnswers

                .map(value => this.normalize(value))

                .sort();

        const received =

            answers

                .map(value => this.normalize(value))

                .sort();

        if (expected.length !== received.length) {

            return false;

        }

        for (let i = 0; i < expected.length; i++) {

            if (expected[i] !== received[i]) {

                return false;

            }

        }

        return true;

    }

    normalize(value) {

        if (value === undefined || value === null) {

            return "";

        }

        value = String(value).trim();

        if (!this.caseSensitive) {

            value = value.toLowerCase();

        }

        return value;

    }

}

export class DifficultyController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentDifficulty = "easy";

        this.thresholds = {

            easy: 0,

            medium: 10,

            hard: 25,

            expert: 50

        };

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("DifficultyController initialisé.");

    }

    update() {

        const level =

            this.game.playerProfile?.level ?? 1;

        const successRate =

            this.game.playerStats?.getSuccessRate?.() ?? 0;

        if (level >= this.thresholds.expert && successRate >= 90) {

            this.currentDifficulty = "expert";

            return;

        }

        if (level >= this.thresholds.hard && successRate >= 75) {

            this.currentDifficulty = "hard";

            return;

        }

        if (level >= this.thresholds.medium && successRate >= 60) {

            this.currentDifficulty = "medium";

            return;

        }

        this.currentDifficulty = "easy";

    }

    getDifficulty() {

        return this.currentDifficulty;

    }

    setDifficulty(difficulty) {

        this.currentDifficulty = difficulty;

    }

    reset() {

        this.currentDifficulty = "easy";

    }

}

export class QuestionSelector {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.random = Math.random;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuestionSelector initialisé.");

    }

    select(source = null) {

        const database =

            this.game.questionDatabase?.getQuestions(

                source

            ) ?? [];

        if (database.length === 0) {

            return [];

        }

        const difficulty =

            this.game.difficultyController?.getDifficulty()

            ?? "easy";

        const filtered = database.filter(

            question =>

                !question.difficulty ||

                question.difficulty === difficulty

        );

        const shuffled =

            [...filtered].sort(

                () => this.random() - 0.5

            );

        const count =

            this.game.config?.quiz?.questionsPerQuiz

            ?? 5;

        return shuffled.slice(0, count);

    }

    selectByCategory(category) {

        return this.select(category);

    }

    selectDailyQuestions() {

        return this.select("daily");

    }

    selectChallengeQuestions() {

        return this.select("challenge");

    }

}

export class QuizController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentSession = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizController initialisé.");

    }

    startQuiz(source) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return false;

        }

        if (this.currentSession) {

            return false;

        }

        const session = this.game.quizSession;

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        session.reset();

        session.setSource(source);

        session.start();

        this.currentSession = session;

        this.game.uiManager?.openQuiz(session);

        return true;

    }

    submitAnswer(answer) {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return false;

        }

        const result =

            this.currentSession.submitAnswer(

                answer

            );

        if (result.correct) {

            this.game.quizRewards?.grant(

                result

            );

        }

        if (result.finished) {

            this.finishQuiz();

        }

        return result;

    }

    finishQuiz() {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentSession.finish();

        this.game.uiManager?.closeQuiz();

        this.currentSession = null;

    }

    cancelQuiz() {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentSession.cancel();

        this.game.uiManager?.closeQuiz();

        this.currentSession = null;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentSession.update(

            deltaTime

        );

    }

}

export class QuizRewards {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.rewardMultiplier = 1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizRewards initialisé.");

    }

    grant(result) {

        if (!result) {

            return;

        }

        const xp = this.calculateXP(result);

        const coins = this.calculateCoins(result);

        this.game.playerProfile?.addExperience(

            xp

        );

        this.game.playerInventory?.addCoins(

            coins

        );

        this.game.achievementManager?.checkQuizAchievements(

            result

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "quiz:reward",

            {

                xp,

                coins,

                score: result.score

            }

        );

        return {

            xp,

            coins

        };

    }

    calculateXP(result) {

        const baseXP =

            result.score * 10;

        return Math.round(

            baseXP * this.rewardMultiplier

        );

    }

    calculateCoins(result) {

        const baseCoins =

            result.score * 5;

        return Math.round(

            baseCoins * this.rewardMultiplier

        );

    }

    setMultiplier(multiplier) {

        this.rewardMultiplier =

            Math.max(

                0,

                multiplier

            );

    }

    reset() {

        this.rewardMultiplier = 1;

    }

}

C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\gameplay\quiz\QuizSession.js 
================================================== 
export class QuizSession {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.active = false;

        this.source = null;

        this.questions = [];

        this.currentIndex = 0;

        this.score = 0;

        this.correctAnswers = 0;

        this.wrongAnswers = 0;

        this.startTime = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizSession initialisée.");

    }

    setSource(source) {

        this.source = source;

    }

    reset() {

        this.active = false;

        this.questions = [];

        this.currentIndex = 0;

        this.score = 0;

        this.correctAnswers = 0;

        this.wrongAnswers = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    start() {

        this.questions =

            this.game.questionSelector?.select(

                this.source

            ) ?? [];

        this.active = true;

        this.startTime = performance.now();

    }

    getCurrentQuestion() {

        return this.questions[

            this.currentIndex

        ] ?? null;

    }

    submitAnswer(answer) {

        const question =

            this.getCurrentQuestion();

        if (!question) {

            return {

                correct: false,

                finished: true

            };

        }

        const correct =

            this.game.answerController?.check(

                question,

                answer

            ) ?? false;

        if (correct) {

            this.correctAnswers++;

            this.score +=

                question.points ?? 1;

        } else {

            this.wrongAnswers++;

        }

        this.currentIndex++;

        return {

            correct,

            finished:

                this.currentIndex >=

                this.questions.length,

            score: this.score,

            currentQuestion:

                this.getCurrentQuestion()

        };

    }

    finish() {

        this.active = false;

    }

    cancel() {

        this.active = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

    }

}

export class LeaderboardController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.entries = [];

        this.maxEntries = 100;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LeaderboardController initialisé.");

    }

    addEntry(playerId, playerName, score) {

        const existing = this.entries.find(

            entry => entry.playerId === playerId

        );

        if (existing) {

            existing.score = Math.max(

                existing.score,

                score

            );

        } else {

            this.entries.push({

                playerId,

                playerName,

                score

            });

        }

        this.sort();

        this.trim();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "leaderboard:updated",

            this.entries

        );

    }

    sort() {

        this.entries.sort(

            (a, b) => b.score - a.score

        );

    }

    trim() {

        if (this.entries.length > this.maxEntries) {

            this.entries.length = this.maxEntries;

        }

    }

    getEntries() {

        return [...this.entries];

    }

    getPlayerRank(playerId) {

        return this.entries.findIndex(

            entry => entry.playerId === playerId

        ) + 1;

    }

    clear() {

        this.entries.length = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // Synchronisation réseau
        // Classement mondial
        // Classement hebdomadaire
        // Classement par mode de jeu

    }

}

export class ScoreController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.score = 0;

        this.bestScore = 0;

        this.multiplier = 1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ScoreController initialisé.");

    }

    addScore(points) {

        if (points <= 0) {

            return;

        }

        const finalScore = Math.floor(

            points * this.multiplier

        );

        this.score += finalScore;

        if (this.score > this.bestScore) {

            this.bestScore = this.score;

        }

        this.game.leaderboardController?.addEntry(

            this.game.localPlayer?.id,

            this.game.localPlayer?.name,

            this.score

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "score:changed",

            {

                score: this.score,

                bestScore: this.bestScore,

                added: finalScore

            }

        );

    }

    removeScore(points) {

        if (points <= 0) {

            return;

        }

        this.score = Math.max(

            0,

            this.score - points

        );

    }

    setMultiplier(multiplier) {

        this.multiplier = Math.max(

            1,

            multiplier

        );

    }

    resetMultiplier() {

        this.multiplier = 1;

    }

    getScore() {

        return this.score;

    }

    getBestScore() {

        return this.bestScore;

    }

    reset() {

        this.score = 0;

        this.multiplier = 1;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // Bonus temporaires
        // Combo
        // Multiplicateurs
        // Synchronisation réseau

    }

}

export class ScoreDisplay {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.lastScore = -1;

        this.lastBestScore = -1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ScoreDisplay initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        const scoreController = this.game.scoreController;

        if (!scoreController) {

            return;

        }

        const score = scoreController.getScore();

        const bestScore = scoreController.getBestScore();

        if (

            score !== this.lastScore ||

            bestScore !== this.lastBestScore

        ) {

            this.lastScore = score;

            this.lastBestScore = bestScore;

            this.refresh();

        }

    }

    refresh() {

        this.game.uiManager?.updateScore?.(

            this.lastScore,

            this.lastBestScore

        );

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

        this.refresh();

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

}

export class ClientStateMachine {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.states = new Map();

        this.currentState = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ClientStateMachine initialisé.");

    }

    register(name, state) {

        this.states.set(

            name,

            state

        );

    }

    changeState(name, data = null) {

        const nextState =

            this.states.get(name);

        if (!nextState) {

            console.warn(

                `État inconnu : ${name}`

            );

            return false;

        }

        if (this.currentState?.exit) {

            this.currentState.exit();

        }

        this.currentState = nextState;

        if (this.currentState.enter) {

            this.currentState.enter(data);

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "state:changed",

            {

                state: name,

                data

            }

        );

        return true;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.currentState?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

    }

    render() {

        this.currentState?.render?.();

    }

    getCurrentState() {

        return this.currentState;

    }

}

export class LobbyState {

                "playing"

            );

        this.game.uiManager?.hideLobby?.();

    }

    setReadyPlayers(count) {

        this.readyPlayers = Math.max(

            0,

            count

        );

    }

        this.requiredPlayers = Math.max(

            1,

            count

        );

    }

}

export class PlayingState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.paused = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("PlayingState initialisé.");

    }

    enter(data = null) {

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.paused = false;

        this.game.uiManager?.showGameplay?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "gameplay:started",

            data

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (this.paused) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        this.game.world?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.game.playerManager?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.game.entityManager?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        if (

            this.game.matchController?.isFinished?.()

        ) {

            this.game.clientStateMachine?.changeState(

                "victory"

            );

        }

    }

    exit() {

        this.game.uiManager?.hideGameplay?.();

    }

    pause() {

        this.paused = true;

    }

    resume() {

        this.paused = false;

    }

    isPaused() {

        return this.paused;

    }

    getElapsedTime() {

        return this.elapsedTime;

    }

}

export class RestartState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.restartDelay = 3;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RestartState initialisé.");

    }

    enter(data = null) {

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.game.uiManager?.showRestartScreen?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "restart:entered",

            data

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        if (

            this.elapsedTime >= this.restartDelay

        ) {

            this.resetGame();

            this.game.clientStateMachine?.changeState(

                "lobby"

            );

        }

    }

    exit() {

        this.game.uiManager?.hideRestartScreen?.();

    }

    resetGame() {

        this.game.scoreController?.reset?.();

        this.game.progressionController?.reset?.();

        this.game.playerManager?.reset?.();

        this.game.entityManager?.reset?.();

        this.game.world?.reset?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "game:reset"

        );

    }

    setRestartDelay(seconds) {

        this.restartDelay = Math.max(

            0,

            seconds

        );

    }

}
