import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChunkRenderer {

    createTerrainMesh(chunk) {

    if (
        !chunk ||
        !chunk.terrain ||
        chunk.terrain.length === 0
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const size =
        chunk.size;


    /*
     * Nombre maximum de blocs
     * potentiellement présents.
     */

    let grassCount = 0;
    let dirtCount = 0;
    let stoneCount = 0;


    /*
     * Première passe :
     * calculer combien de blocs
     * appartiennent à chaque matériau.
     */

    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            const rawHeight =
                Number(
                    chunk.terrain[localZ]?.[localX]
                );

            if (
                !Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            const height =
    Math.max(
        1,
        Math.floor(rawHeight) + 1
    );


for (
    let y = 0;
    y < height;
    y++
) {

    if (
        chunk.isBlockRemoved(
            localX,
            y,
            localZ
        )
    ) {

        continue;

    }


    if (
        y === height - 1
    ) {

        grassCount += 1;

    }
    else if (
        y >= height - 4
    ) {

        dirtCount += 1;

    }
    else {

        stoneCount += 1;

    }

} ;}}


    /*
     * Groupe contenant tout le terrain
     * du chunk.
     */

    const group =
        new THREE.Group();

    group.name =
        `terrain_${chunk.key}`;


    /*
     * Géométrie commune.
     */

    const blockGeometry =
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(
            1,
            1,
            1
        );


    /*
     * Matériaux.
     */

    const grassMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x6ea84a,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });

    const dirtMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x8d6e63,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });

    const stoneMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x777777,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    /*
     * Un seul InstancedMesh par matériau.
     */

    const grassMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            grassMaterial,
            grassCount
        );

    const dirtMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            dirtMaterial,
            dirtCount
        );

    const stoneMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            stoneMaterial,
            stoneCount
        );


    grassMesh.castShadow = true;
    grassMesh.receiveShadow = true;

    dirtMesh.castShadow = true;
    dirtMesh.receiveShadow = true;

    stoneMesh.castShadow = true;
    stoneMesh.receiveShadow = true;


    const matrix =
        new THREE.Matrix4();


    let grassIndex = 0;
    let dirtIndex = 0;
    let stoneIndex = 0;


    /*
     * Deuxième passe :
     * positionner réellement les blocs.
     */

    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            const rawHeight =
                Number(
                    chunk.terrain[localZ]?.[localX]
                );

            if (
                !Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            const height =
                Math.max(
                    1,
                    Math.floor(rawHeight) + 1
                );


            for (
                let y = 0;
                y < height;
                y++
            ) {
                if (
    chunk.isBlockRemoved(
        localX,
        y,
        localZ
    )
) {

    continue;

}

                matrix.makeTranslation(
                    localX + 0.5,
                    y + 0.5,
                    localZ + 0.5
                );


                if (
                    y === height - 1
                ) {

                    grassMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        grassIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    grassIndex++;

                }
                else if (
                    y >= height - 4
                ) {

                    dirtMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        dirtIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    dirtIndex++;

                }
                else {

                    stoneMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        stoneIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    stoneIndex++;
                }
            }
        }
    }


    /*
     * Mise à jour des matrices GPU.
     */

    grassMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;

    dirtMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;

    stoneMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;


    /*
     * Informations utiles pour les
     * futures collisions.
     */

    grassMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "grass",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };

    dirtMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "dirt",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };

    stoneMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "stone",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    /*
     * Ajouter uniquement les meshes
     * réellement nécessaires.
     */

    if (grassCount > 0) {
        group.add(grassMesh);
    }

    if (dirtCount > 0) {
        group.add(dirtMesh);
    }

    if (stoneCount > 0) {
        group.add(stoneMesh);
    }


    group.userData = {
        type: "voxelTerrain",
        chunkKey: chunk.key,
        size
    };


    return group;
}

getBiomeColor(biome) {

    switch (biome) {

        case "desert":

            return 0xd9c27a;

        case "forest":

            return 0x3f8c3a;

        case "plains":

            return 0x6ea84a;

        default:

            return 0x6ea84a;

    }

}

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.renderedChunks = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene =
            this.game.scene.getScene();

        console.log(
            "ChunkRenderer initialisé."
        );

    }

    render(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }

    if (
        this.renderedChunks.has(
            chunk.key
        )
    ) {

        return;

    }

    if (
        chunk.terrain &&
        chunk.meshes.length === 0
    ) {

        const terrainMesh =
            this.createTerrainMesh(
                chunk
            );

        if (terrainMesh) {

            chunk.addMesh(
                terrainMesh
            );

        }

    }

    this.scene.add(
        chunk.group
    );

    chunk.setVisible(true);

    chunk.markLoaded();

    this.renderedChunks.set(
        chunk.key,
        chunk
    );

}
rebuild(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }


    /*
     * Supprimer l'ancien mesh du terrain.
     */

    for (
        const mesh of chunk.meshes
    ) {

        chunk.group.remove(mesh);


        mesh.traverse?.(
            object => {

                if (object.geometry) {

                    object.geometry.dispose();

                }


                if (object.material) {

                    if (
                        Array.isArray(
                            object.material
                        )
                    ) {

                        object.material.forEach(
                            material => {

                                material.dispose();

                            }
                        );

                    }
                    else {

                        object.material.dispose();

                    }

                }

            }
        );

    }


    chunk.meshes.length = 0;


    /*
     * Recréer le terrain avec les
     * blocs actuellement présents.
     */

    const terrainMesh =
        this.createTerrainMesh(
            chunk
        );


    if (terrainMesh) {

        chunk.addMesh(
            terrainMesh
        );

    }

}

    hide(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        chunk.setVisible(false);

    }

    show(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        chunk.setVisible(true);

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        if (
            !this.renderedChunks.has(
                chunk.key
            )
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.scene.remove(
            chunk.group
        );

        this.renderedChunks.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    isRendered(chunk) {

        return this.renderedChunks.has(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    clear() {

        if (!this.scene) {
            return;
        }

        for (
            const chunk of
            this.renderedChunks.values()
        ) {

            this.scene.remove(
                chunk.group
            );

        }

        this.renderedChunks.clear();

    }

    update() {

        // Réservé aux futures optimisations :
        // - GPU Culling
        // - Occlusion Culling
        // - Distance Rendering
        // - Instancing dynamique

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clear();

        this.scene = null;

        this.game = null;

    }

}

export class ChunkUnloader {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "ChunkUnloader initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        const player =
            this.game.localPlayer;

        if (!player) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize;

        const unloadDistance =
            this.game.config?.world?.unloadDistance;

        /*
         * Vérifier la configuration.
         */

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(unloadDistance) ||
            unloadDistance < 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Calculer le chunk dans lequel
         * se trouve actuellement le joueur.
         */

        const playerChunkX =
            Math.floor(
                player.position.x / chunkSize
            );

        const playerChunkZ =
            Math.floor(
                player.position.z / chunkSize
            );

        /*
         * Récupérer les chunks actuellement
         * chargés.
         */

        const loadedChunks =
            this.chunkManager.getLoadedChunks();

        /*
         * Créer une liste des chunks à retirer.
         *
         * On ne les supprime pas directement
         * pendant le parcours de la Map.
         */

        const chunksToRemove = [];

        for (
            const chunk of loadedChunks.values()
        ) {

            if (!chunk) {
                continue;
            }

            const dx =
                chunk.chunkX -
                playerChunkX;

            const dz =
                chunk.chunkZ -
                playerChunkZ;

            /*
             * Distance en grille.
             *
             * Exemple :
             *
             * joueur = 0:0
             * chunk  = 0:5
             *
             * distance = 5
             */

            const distance =
                Math.max(
                    Math.abs(dx),
                    Math.abs(dz)
                );

            /*
             * Le chunk est encore assez proche.
             */

            if (
                distance <= unloadDistance
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            chunksToRemove.push({
                chunkX: chunk.chunkX,
                chunkZ: chunk.chunkZ
            });

        }

        /*
         * Supprimer maintenant les chunks
         * qui sont trop éloignés.
         */

        for (
            const coordinates of chunksToRemove
        ) {

            this.chunkManager.removeChunk(
                coordinates.chunkX,
                coordinates.chunkZ
            );

        }

    }

    destroy() {

        this.chunkManager = null;

    }

}

================================================== 
export class ChunkWorker {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.worker = null;

        this.enabled = false;

        this.callbacks = new Map();

        this.requestId = 0;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        /*
         * Pour le moment, aucun Worker réel
         * n'est lancé ici.
         *
         * La génération reste exécutée
         * côté client principal.
         *
         * Cela évite de charger ce même fichier
         * comme Worker et de créer une boucle
         * de Worker incorrecte.
         */

        this.worker = null;

        this.enabled = false;

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "ChunkWorker initialisé en mode fallback."
        );

    }

    generateChunk(data) {

        /*
         * Aucun Worker actif :
         * retourner directement les données.
         */

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            !this.worker
        ) {

            return Promise.resolve(
                data
            );

        }

        return new Promise(
            (resolve, reject) => {

                const id =
                    ++this.requestId;

                this.callbacks.set(
                    id,
                    {
                        resolve,
                        reject
                    }
                );

                try {

                    this.worker.postMessage({

                        id,

                        type:
                            "GENERATE_CHUNK",

                        payload:
                            data

                    });

                }
                catch (error) {

                    this.callbacks.delete(
                        id
                    );

                    reject(error);

                }

            }
        );

    }

    onMessage(event) {

        if (!event?.data) {

            return;

        }

        const {

            id,

            payload,

            error

        } = event.data;

        const callback =
            this.callbacks.get(id);

        if (!callback) {

            return;

        }

        this.callbacks.delete(id);

        if (error) {

            callback.reject(
                new Error(error)
            );

            return;

        }

        callback.resolve(
            payload
        );

    }

    onError(error) {

        for (
            const callback
            of this.callbacks.values()
        ) {

            callback.reject(
                error
            );

        }

        this.callbacks.clear();

    }

    terminate() {

        if (this.worker) {

            this.worker.terminate();

        }

        this.worker = null;

        for (
            const callback
            of this.callbacks.values()
        ) {

            callback.reject(
                new Error(
                    "ChunkWorker terminated."
                )
            );

        }

        this.callbacks.clear();

        this.enabled = false;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

import { TreeManager } from "./TreeManager.js";
import { StructureManager } from "./StructureManager.js";
import { ObjectManager } from "./ObjectManager.js";

export class EnvironmentManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.treeManager = null;

        this.structureManager = null;

        this.objectManager = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.treeManager =
            new TreeManager(this.game);

        await this.treeManager.initialize();

        this.structureManager =
            new StructureManager(this.game);

        await this.structureManager.initialize();

        this.objectManager =
            new ObjectManager(this.game);

        await this.objectManager.initialize();

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "EnvironmentManager initialisé."
        );

    }

    generateChunk(chunk) {

        if (
            !this.initialized ||
            !chunk
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Générer les arbres.
         */

        this.treeManager?.generate(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Générer les structures.
         */

        this.structureManager?.generate(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Générer les autres objets.
         */

        this.objectManager?.generate(
            chunk
        );

    }

    unloadChunk(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Nettoyer les arbres.
         */

        this.treeManager?.remove(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Nettoyer les structures.
         */

        this.structureManager?.remove(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Nettoyer les autres objets.
         */

        this.objectManager?.remove(
            chunk
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.initialized) {

            return;

        }

        this.treeManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

        this.structureManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

        this.objectManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

    }

    destroy() {

        /*
         * Nettoyage général.
         */

        this.treeManager = null;

        this.structureManager = null;

        this.objectManager = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

export class InteractiveEnvironment {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.interactives = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "InteractiveEnvironment initialisé."
        );

    }

    register(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return null;

        }

        const id =
            object.userData?.id ??
            crypto.randomUUID();

        /*
         * Enregistrer l'identifiant
         * directement sur l'objet.
         */

        if (!object.userData) {

            object.userData = {};

        }

        object.userData.id = id;

        this.interactives.set(
            id,
            object
        );

        return id;

    }

    unregister(id) {

        if (!id) {

            return false;

        }

        return this.interactives.delete(
            id
        );

    }

    get(id) {

        if (!id) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.interactives.get(
            id
        ) ?? null;

    }

    getNearby(position, radius = 3) {

        if (!position) {

            return [];

        }

        const numericRadius =
            Number(radius);

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(
                numericRadius
            ) ||
            numericRadius < 0
        ) {

            return [];

        }

        const results = [];

        const radiusSquared =
            numericRadius *
            numericRadius;

        for (
            const object of
            this.interactives.values()
        ) {

            if (!object?.position) {

                continue;

            }

            const distanceSquared =
                position.distanceToSquared(
                    object.position
                );

            if (
                distanceSquared <=
                radiusSquared
            ) {

                results.push(
                    object
                );

            }

        }

        return results;

    }

    update(playerPosition) {

        if (!playerPosition) {

            this.game.nearbyInteractives = [];

            return;

        }

        const nearby =
            this.getNearby(
                playerPosition,
                3
            );

        this.game.nearbyInteractives =
            nearby;

    }

    clear() {

        this.interactives.clear();

        if (this.game) {

            this.game.nearbyInteractives =
                [];

        }

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clear();

        this.game = null;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ObjectManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkObjects = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ObjectManager initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !chunk.objects) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Éviter de générer deux fois
         * les objets du même chunk.
         */

        if (this.chunkObjects.has(chunk.key)) {

            return;

        }

        const objects = [];

        for (const object of chunk.objects) {

            if (
                object.type === "tree" ||
                object.type === "smallTree"
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            const mesh =
                this.createObject(object);

            if (!mesh) {

                continue;

            }

            objects.push(mesh);

            /*
             * Le mesh appartient maintenant
             * directement au chunk.
             */

            chunk.add(mesh);

        }

        this.chunkObjects.set(

            chunk.key,

            objects

        );

    }

    createObject(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return null;

        }

        let geometry;

        let material;

        switch (object.type) {

            case "rock":

                geometry =
                    new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(
                        0.7,
                        0
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0x7a7a7a

                    });

                break;

            case "bush":

                geometry =
                    new THREE.SphereGeometry(
                        0.6,
                        10,
                        10
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0x3e8d35

                    });

                break;

            case "cactus":

                geometry =
                    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(
                        0.25,
                        0.3,
                        2,
                        8
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0x2d8a43

                    });

                break;

            default:

                geometry =
                    new THREE.BoxGeometry(
                        0.6,
                        0.6,
                        0.6
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0xffffff

                    });

                break;

        }

        const mesh =
            new THREE.Mesh(
                geometry,
                material
            );

        mesh.position.copy(
            object.position
        );

        mesh.userData = {

            id: object.id,

            type: object.type,

            biome: object.biome

        };

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return;

        }

        const objects =
            this.chunkObjects.get(
                chunk.key
            );

        if (!objects) {

            return;

        }

        for (const object of objects) {

            chunk.remove(object);

            this.disposeObject(object);

        }

        this.chunkObjects.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    disposeObject(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return;

        }

        object.traverse(child => {

            if (child.geometry) {

                child.geometry.dispose();

            }

            if (child.material) {

                if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                    child.material.forEach(
                        material => {

                            material.dispose();

                        }
                    );

                }

                else {

                    child.material.dispose();

                }

            }

        });

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * Futures améliorations :
         *
         * - animations
         * - rotation
         * - physique
         * - effets
         * - instancing
         * - LOD
         */

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class StructureManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkStructures = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("StructureManager initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !chunk.structures) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Ne pas générer plusieurs fois
         * les structures du même chunk.
         */

        if (this.chunkStructures.has(chunk.key)) {

            return;

        }

        const structures = [];

        for (const data of chunk.structures) {

            const structure =
                this.createStructure(data);

            if (!structure) {

                continue;

            }

            structures.push(structure);

            /*
             * La structure appartient
             * directement au chunk.
             */

            chunk.add(structure);

        }

        this.chunkStructures.set(

            chunk.key,

            structures

        );

    }

    createStructure(data) {

        if (!data) {

            return null;

        }

        const group =
            new THREE.Group();

        group.name =
            data.id ??
            "structure";

        const baseGeometry =
            new THREE.BoxGeometry(
                4,
                2,
                4
            );

        const baseMaterial =
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                color: 0x8d6e63

            });

        const base =
            new THREE.Mesh(
                baseGeometry,
                baseMaterial
            );

        base.position.y = 1;

        base.castShadow = true;

        base.receiveShadow = true;

        group.add(base);

        const roofGeometry =
            new THREE.ConeGeometry(
                3.2,
                2,
                4
            );

        const roofMaterial =
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                color: 0x5d4037

            });

        const roof =
            new THREE.Mesh(
                roofGeometry,
                roofMaterial
            );

        roof.position.y = 3;

        roof.rotation.y =
            Math.PI / 4;

        roof.castShadow = true;

        roof.receiveShadow = true;

        group.add(roof);

        if (data.position) {

            group.position.copy(
                data.position
            );

        }

        group.rotation.y =
            Number(data.rotation) || 0;

        group.userData = {

            id: data.id,

            type: data.type,

            biome: data.biome

        };

        return group;

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return;

        }

        const structures =
            this.chunkStructures.get(
                chunk.key
            );

        if (!structures) {

            return;

        }

        for (const structure of structures) {

            chunk.remove(structure);

            this.disposeObject(structure);

        }

        this.chunkStructures.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    disposeObject(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return;

        }

        object.traverse(child => {

            if (child.geometry) {

                child.geometry.dispose();

            }

            if (child.material) {

                if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                    child.material.forEach(
                        material => {

                            material.dispose();

                        }
                    );

                }

                else {

                    child.material.dispose();

                }

            }

        });

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * Futures améliorations :
         *
         * - animations
         * - portes
         * - destruction
         * - interactions
         * - LOD
         * - streaming avancé
         */

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class TreeManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkTrees = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("TreeManager initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

    if (!chunk || !chunk.objects) {

        return;

    }

    const trees = [];

    const chunkWorldX =
        chunk.chunkX * chunk.size;

    const chunkWorldZ =
        chunk.chunkZ * chunk.size;

    for (const object of chunk.objects) {

        if (
            object.type !== "tree" &&
            object.type !== "smallTree"
        ) {

            continue;

        }

        const tree =
            this.createTree(object);

        tree.position.x -= chunkWorldX;

        tree.position.z -= chunkWorldZ;

        trees.push(tree);

        chunk.add(tree);

    }

    this.chunkTrees.set(
        chunk.key,
        trees
    );

}

    createTree(object) {

        const group = new THREE.Group();

        const trunkGeometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(

            0.25,
            0.35,
            2,
            8

        );

        const trunkMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x6d4c41

        });

        const trunk = new THREE.Mesh(

            trunkGeometry,

            trunkMaterial

        );

        trunk.position.y = 1;

        group.add(trunk);

        const leavesGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(

            1.2,

            12,

            12

        );

        const leavesMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x2e7d32

        });

        const leaves = new THREE.Mesh(

            leavesGeometry,

            leavesMaterial

        );

        leaves.position.y = 2.6;

        group.add(leaves);

        group.position.copy(

            object.position

        );

        group.userData.type = "tree";

        group.userData.id = object.id;

        return group;

    }

    remove(chunk) {

    const trees =
        this.chunkTrees.get(
            chunk.key
        );

    if (!trees) {

        return;

    }

    for (const tree of trees) {

        chunk.remove(tree);

        this.disposeObject(tree);

    }

    this.chunkTrees.delete(
        chunk.key
    );

}

disposeObject(object) {

    object.traverse(child => {

        if (child.geometry) {

            child.geometry.dispose();

        }

        if (child.material) {

            if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                child.material.forEach(
                    material => material.dispose()
                );

            }

            else {

                child.material.dispose();

            }

        }

    });

}

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Animations futures :
        // vent
        // saisons
        // destruction
        // croissance

    }

}

import { NoiseGenerator } from "./NoiseGenerator.js";

export class BiomeGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.noise = null;

        this.moistureFrequency = 0.003;

        this.temperatureFrequency = 0.002;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.noise =
            new NoiseGenerator(
                this.game
            );

        await this.noise.initialize();

        this.moistureFrequency =
            Number(
                this.game.config?.world?.moistureFrequency ?? 0.003
            );

        this.temperatureFrequency =
            Number(
                this.game.config?.world?.temperatureFrequency ?? 0.002
            );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.moistureFrequency) ||
            this.moistureFrequency <= 0
        ) {

            this.moistureFrequency = 0.003;

        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.temperatureFrequency) ||
            this.temperatureFrequency <= 0
        ) {

            this.temperatureFrequency = 0.002;

        }

        console.log(
            "BiomeGenerator initialisé."
        );

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return "plains";

        }

        const chunkSize =
            Number(
                this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize
            );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {

            return "plains";

        }

        const worldX =
            chunk.chunkX *
            chunkSize;

        const worldZ =
            chunk.chunkZ *
            chunkSize;

        return this.getBiome(
            worldX,
            worldZ
        );

    }

    getBiome(worldX, worldZ) {

        if (!this.noise) {

            return "plains";

        }

        /*
         * NoiseGenerator.sample() applique déjà
         * sa fréquence interne.
         *
         * On compense cette fréquence ici afin
         * que les fréquences spécifiques aux biomes
         * soient appliquées exactement une seule fois.
         */

        const noiseFrequency =
            Number(
                this.noise.frequency
            );

        const baseFrequency =
            Number.isFinite(noiseFrequency) &&
            noiseFrequency > 0
                ? noiseFrequency
                : 0.01;

        const moistureX =
            (
                worldX *
                this.moistureFrequency
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const moistureZ =
            (
                worldZ *
                this.moistureFrequency
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const temperatureX =
            (
                worldX *
                this.temperatureFrequency +
                5000
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const temperatureZ =
            (
                worldZ *
                this.temperatureFrequency +
                5000
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const moisture =
            this.noise.sample(
                moistureX,
                moistureZ
            );

        const temperature =
            this.noise.sample(
                temperatureX,
                temperatureZ
            );

        if (
            temperature > 0.65 &&
            moisture < 0.35
        ) {

            return "desert";

        }

        if (
            moisture > 0.60
        ) {

            return "forest";

        }

        return "plains";

    }

}

import { NoiseGenerator } from "./NoiseGenerator.js";

export class HeightGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.noise = null;

        this.chunkSize = 0;

        this.baseHeight = 0;

        this.heightScale = 4;

        this.frequency = 0.01;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize ?? 16;

        this.baseHeight =
            this.game.config?.world?.baseHeight ?? 0;

        this.heightScale =
            this.game.config?.world?.heightScale ?? 4;

        this.frequency =
            this.game.config?.world?.terrainFrequency ?? 0.005;

        this.noise =
            new NoiseGenerator(this.game);

        await this.noise.initialize();

        console.log(
            "HeightGenerator initialisé."
        );

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !this.noise) {

            return [];

        }

        const terrain = [];

        const worldStartX =
            chunk.chunkX * this.chunkSize;

        const worldStartZ =
            chunk.chunkZ * this.chunkSize;

        for (
            let z = 0;
            z < this.chunkSize;
            z++
        ) {

            const row = [];

            for (
                let x = 0;
                x < this.chunkSize;
                x++
            ) {

                const worldX =
                    worldStartX + x;

                const worldZ =
                    worldStartZ + z;

                const noise =
                    this.noise.sample(
                        worldX * this.frequency,
                        worldZ * this.frequency
                    );

                const height =
                    this.baseHeight +
                    noise * this.heightScale;

                row.push(height);

            }

            terrain.push(row);

        }

        return terrain;

    }

    getHeight(terrain, localX, localZ) {

        if (
            !Array.isArray(terrain) ||
            terrain.length === 0
        ) {

            return 0;

        }

        const z =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    terrain.length - 1,
                    Math.floor(localZ)
                )
            );

        const row = terrain[z];

        if (
            !Array.isArray(row) ||
            row.length === 0
        ) {

            return 0;

        }

        const x =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    row.length - 1,
                    Math.floor(localX)
                )
            );

        return row[x];

    }

}

C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\world\generation\NoiseGenerator.js 
================================================== 
export class NoiseGenerator {

constructor(game) {

    this.game = game;

    this.seed = 0;

    this.frequency = 0.01;

    this.octaves = 4;

    this.persistence = 0.5;

    this.lacunarity = 2.0;

}

async initialize() {

    const worldSeed =
        this.game.worldManager?.getSeed?.();

    this.seed =
        worldSeed ??
        this.game.config?.world?.seed ??
        12345;

    this.frequency =
        this.game.config?.world?.noiseFrequency ?? 0.01;

    this.octaves =
        Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(
                Number(
                    this.game.config?.world?.noiseOctaves ?? 4
                )
            )
        );

    this.persistence =
        Number(
            this.game.config?.world?.noisePersistence ?? 0.5
        );

    this.lacunarity =
        Number(
            this.game.config?.world?.noiseLacunarity ?? 2.0
        );

    console.log("NoiseGenerator initialisé.");

}

sample(x, z) {

    let amplitude = 1;

    let frequency = this.frequency;

    let value = 0;

    let maxAmplitude = 0;

    for (
        let octave = 0;
        octave < this.octaves;
        octave++
    ) {

        value +=
            this.random2D(
                x * frequency,
                z * frequency
            ) * amplitude;

        maxAmplitude += amplitude;

        amplitude *= this.persistence;

        frequency *= this.lacunarity;

    }

    if (maxAmplitude <= 0) {

        return 0.5;

    }

    value /= maxAmplitude;

    return Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
            1,
            (value + 1) * 0.5
        )
    );

}

random2D(x, z) {

    const n =
        Math.sin(

            x * 127.1 +

            z * 311.7 +

            this.seed * 74.7

        ) * 43758.5453123;

    return (
        n - Math.floor(n)
    ) * 2 - 1;

}

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ObjectGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkSize = 0;

        this.seed = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize ?? 16;

        this.seed =
            this.game.worldSeed?.getSeed?.() ??
            this.game.config?.world?.seed ??
            12345;

        console.log(
            "ObjectGenerator initialisé."
        );

    }

    generate(chunk, terrain, biomeId) {

        if (!chunk || !terrain || !biomeId) {

            return;

        }

        const biome =
            this.game.worldManager
                ?.getBiomeManager?.()
                ?.get?.(biomeId);

        /*
         * Pour l'instant, on récupère les données
         * directement depuis la configuration
         * du biome si le BiomeManager n'est pas
         * exposé par WorldManager.
         */

        let biomeData = null;

        if (biome?.getData) {

            biomeData = biome.getData();

        }

        if (!biomeData) {

            biomeData =
                this.getFallbackBiomeData(
                    biomeId
                );

        }

        const objects = [];

        const objectConfig =
            biomeData.objects;

        if (
            !objectConfig ||
            !Array.isArray(objectConfig.types)
        ) {

            chunk.objects = objects;

            return;

        }

        const density =
            Number(objectConfig.density) || 0;

        if (density <= 0) {

            chunk.objects = objects;

            return;

        }

        const attempts =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.floor(
                    this.chunkSize *
                    this.chunkSize *
                    density
                )
            );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < attempts;
            i++
        ) {

            const random =
                this.random(
                    chunk,
                    i
                );

            const localX =
                Math.floor(
                    random.x *
                    this.chunkSize
                );

            const localZ =
                Math.floor(
                    random.z *
                    this.chunkSize
                );

            const height =
                terrain[
                    Math.min(
                        localZ,
                        terrain.length - 1
                    )
                ]?.[
                    Math.min(
                        localX,
                        terrain[0].length - 1
                    )
                ];

            if (!Number.isFinite(height)) {

                continue;

            }

            const typeIndex =
                Math.floor(
                    random.type *
                    objectConfig.types.length
                );

            const type =
                objectConfig.types[
                    Math.min(
                        typeIndex,
                        objectConfig.types.length - 1
                    )
                ];

            objects.push({

                id:
                    `object_${chunk.key}_${i}`,

                type,

                biome: biomeId,

                position:
                    new THREE.Vector3(

                        chunk.chunkX *
                            this.chunkSize +
                            localX,

                        height,

                        chunk.chunkZ *
                            this.chunkSize +
                            localZ

                    ),

                generated: true

            });

        }

        chunk.objects = objects;

    }

    getFallbackBiomeData(biomeId) {

        switch (biomeId) {

            case "desert":

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0.08,

                        types: [
                            "rock",
                            "deadBush",
                            "cactus"
                        ]

                    }

                };

            case "forest":

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0.35,

                        types: [
                            "tree",
                            "rock",
                            "bush",
                            "log"
                        ]

                    }

                };

            case "plains":

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0.15,

                        types: [
                            "bush",
                            "rock",
                            "smallTree"
                        ]

                    }

                };

            default:

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0,

                        types: []

                    }

                };

        }

    }

    random(chunk, index) {

        const base =
            chunk.chunkX * 928371 +
            chunk.chunkZ * 689917 +
            index * 421;

        const x =
            this.hash(base + 1);

        const z =
            this.hash(base + 2);

        const type =
            this.hash(base + 3);

        return {

            x,
            z,
            type

        };

    }

    hash(value) {

        const n =
            Math.sin(
                value +
                this.seed * 0.0001
            ) *
            43758.5453123;

        return (
            n -
            Math.floor(n)
        );

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class StructureGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkSize = 0;

        this.spawnChance = 0.08;

        this.seed = 987654;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.chunkSize =

            this.game.config.world.chunkSize;

        this.spawnChance =

            this.game.config.world.structureChance ?? 0.08;

        console.log("StructureGenerator initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk, terrain, biome) {

        if (!terrain) {

            chunk.structures = [];

            return;

        }

        const structures = [];

        if (!this.shouldGenerate(chunk, biome)) {

            chunk.structures = structures;

            return;

        }

        const localX = Math.floor(this.chunkSize * 0.5);
        const localZ = Math.floor(this.chunkSize * 0.5);

        const height =

            terrain[
                Math.min(localZ, terrain.length - 1)
            ][
                Math.min(localX, terrain[0].length - 1)
            ];

        structures.push({

            id: `structure_${chunk.key}`,

            type: this.getStructureType(biome),

            biome,

            position: new THREE.Vector3(

                chunk.chunkX * this.chunkSize + localX,

                height,

                chunk.chunkZ * this.chunkSize + localZ

            ),

            rotation:

                this.random(chunk) * Math.PI * 2,

            generated: true

        });

        chunk.structures = structures;

    }

    shouldGenerate(chunk, biome) {

        if (biome === "desert") {

            return this.random(chunk) < this.spawnChance * 0.5;

        }

        if (biome === "forest") {

            return this.random(chunk) < this.spawnChance * 1.2;

        }

        return this.random(chunk) < this.spawnChance;

    }

    getStructureType(biome) {

        switch (biome) {

            case "forest":
                return "forest_ruins";

            case "desert":
                return "desert_ruins";

            case "plains":
                return "camp";

            default:
                return "structure";

        }

    }

    random(chunk) {

        const n = Math.sin(

            chunk.chunkX * 92821 +

            chunk.chunkZ * 68917 +

            this.seed

        ) * 43758.5453123;

        return n - Math.floor(n);

    }

}

import { HeightGenerator } from "./HeightGenerator.js";
import { BiomeGenerator } from "./BiomeGenerator.js";
import { ObjectGenerator } from "./ObjectGenerator.js";
import { StructureGenerator } from "./StructureGenerator.js";

export class TerrainGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.heightGenerator = null;
        this.biomeGenerator = null;
        this.objectGenerator = null;
        this.structureGenerator = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.heightGenerator = new HeightGenerator(this.game);
        await this.heightGenerator.initialize();

        this.biomeGenerator = new BiomeGenerator(this.game);
        await this.biomeGenerator.initialize();

        this.objectGenerator = new ObjectGenerator(this.game);
        await this.objectGenerator.initialize();

        this.structureGenerator = new StructureGenerator(this.game);
        await this.structureGenerator.initialize();

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("TerrainGenerator initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

    if (!this.initialized || !chunk) {

        return false;

    }

    const terrain =
        this.heightGenerator.generate(chunk);

    if (!terrain) {

        return false;

    }

    chunk.terrain = terrain;

    const biome =
        this.biomeGenerator.generate(chunk);

    if (!biome) {

        return false;

    }

    chunk.setBiome(biome);

    this.objectGenerator.generate(
        chunk,
        terrain,
        biome
    );

    this.structureGenerator.generate(
        chunk,
        terrain,
        biome
    );

    chunk.markGenerated();

    return true;

}

}

export class GenerationScheduler {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.queue = [];

        this.running = false;

        this.maxConcurrent = 1;

        this.activeJobs = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.queue = [];

        this.running = false;

        this.activeJobs = 0;

        console.log("GenerationScheduler initialisé.");

    }

    schedule(task, priority = 0) {

        if (typeof task !== "function") {

            return false;

        }

        this.queue.push({

            task,

            priority

        });

        this.queue.sort(

            (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority

        );

        this.process();

        return true;

    }

    process() {

        if (this.running) {

            return;

        }

        this.running = true;

        while (

            this.activeJobs < this.maxConcurrent &&

            this.queue.length > 0

        ) {

            const job = this.queue.shift();

            this.activeJobs++;

            Promise.resolve()

                .then(() => job.task())

                .catch(error => {

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "world:generation-error",

                        error

                    );

                })

                .finally(() => {

                    this.activeJobs--;

                    this.running = false;

                    this.process();

                });

        }

        if (this.activeJobs === 0) {

            this.running = false;

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.queue.length = 0;

    }

    getQueueSize() {

        return this.queue.length;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

}

export class PriorityQueue {

    constructor() {

        this.items = [];

    }

    enqueue(item, priority = 0) {

        this.items.push({

            item,

            priority

        });

        this.items.sort(

            (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority

        );

    }

    dequeue() {

        if (this.items.length === 0) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.items.shift().item;

    }

    peek() {

        if (this.items.length === 0) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.items[0].item;

    }

    remove(item) {

        const index = this.items.findIndex(

            entry => entry.item === item

        );

        if (index === -1) {

            return false;

        }

        this.items.splice(index, 1);

        return true;

    }

    clear() {

        this.items.length = 0;

    }

    size() {

        return this.items.length;

    }

    isEmpty() {

        return this.items.length === 0;

    }

}

export class StreamingRadius {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.radius = 4;

        this.previousRadius = this.radius;

    }

    async initialize() {

        const configuredRadius =

            this.game.config?.world?.renderDistance;

        if (Number.isFinite(configuredRadius)) {

            this.radius = Math.max(

                0,

                Math.floor(configuredRadius)

            );

            this.previousRadius = this.radius;

        }

        console.log("StreamingRadius initialisé.");

    }

    setRadius(radius) {

        const value = Number(radius);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {

            return;

        }

        const nextRadius = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.floor(value)

        );

        if (nextRadius === this.radius) {

            return;

        }

        this.previousRadius = this.radius;

        this.radius = nextRadius;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "world:streaming-radius-changed",

            {

                radius: this.radius,

                previousRadius: this.previousRadius

            }

        );

    }

    getRadius() {

        return this.radius;

    }

    getPreviousRadius() {

        return this.previousRadius;

    }

    getRequiredChunks(centerX, centerZ) {

        const chunks = [];

        for (

            let x = centerX - this.radius;

            x <= centerX + this.radius;

            x++

        ) {

            for (

                let z = centerZ - this.radius;

                z <= centerZ + this.radius;

                z++

            ) {

                chunks.push({

                    x,

                    z

                });

            }

        }

        return chunks;

    }

    contains(centerX, centerZ, chunkX, chunkZ) {

        return (

            Math.abs(chunkX - centerX) <= this.radius &&

            Math.abs(chunkZ - centerZ) <= this.radius

        );

    }

}

export class WorldStreamer {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager ?? null;

        this.streamingRadius = null;

        this.active = false;

        this.lastChunkX = null;

        this.lastChunkZ = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.streamingRadius =
            this.game.streamingRadius ?? null;

        this.active = true;

        console.log(
            "WorldStreamer initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.active) {
            return;
        }

        const player =
            this.game.localPlayer;

        if (
            !player ||
            !this.chunkManager
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkX = Math.floor(
            player.position.x / chunkSize
        );

        const chunkZ = Math.floor(
            player.position.z / chunkSize
        );

        /*
         * Ne recalculer que si le joueur
         * change de chunk.
         */

        if (
            chunkX === this.lastChunkX &&
            chunkZ === this.lastChunkZ
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.lastChunkX = chunkX;

        this.lastChunkZ = chunkZ;

        this.streamAround(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

    }

    streamAround(centerX, centerZ) {

        if (!this.streamingRadius) {
            return;
        }

        const chunks =
    this.streamingRadius
        .getRequiredChunks(
            centerX,
            centerZ
        );

chunks.sort(
    (a, b) => {

        const distanceA =
            Math.max(
                Math.abs(
                    a.x - centerX
                ),
                Math.abs(
                    a.z - centerZ
                )
            );

        const distanceB =
            Math.max(
                Math.abs(
                    b.x - centerX
                ),
                Math.abs(
                    b.z - centerZ
                )
            );

        return distanceA - distanceB;
    }
);

for (
    const coordinates of chunks
) {

            const chunkX =
                coordinates.x;

            const chunkZ =
                coordinates.z;

            if (
                this.chunkManager.hasChunk(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                )
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            this.chunkManager.loadChunk(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(
            "world:streaming-updated",
            {
                centerX,
                centerZ,
                chunks
            }
        );

    }

    stop() {

        this.active = false;

    }

    start() {

        this.active = true;

    }

    isActive() {

        return this.active;

    }

    destroy() {

        this.active = false;

        this.chunkManager = null;

        this.streamingRadius = null;

        this.lastChunkX = null;

        this.lastChunkZ = null;

    }

}

import { Game } from "./src/core/Game.js";

const loadingScreen =
    document.getElementById("loading-screen");

const loadingMessage =
    document.getElementById("loading-message");

const loadingProgress =
    document.getElementById("loading-progress");

function setLoading(
    message,
    progress = null
) {

    if (loadingMessage) {

        loadingMessage.textContent =
            message;

    }

    if (
        loadingProgress &&
        progress !== null
    ) {

        const value =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    100,
                    Number(progress)
                )
            );

        loadingProgress.style.width =
            `${value}%`;

        loadingProgress.setAttribute(
            "aria-valuenow",
            String(value)
        );

    }

}

function hideLoading() {

    if (!loadingScreen) {

        return;

    }

    loadingScreen.classList.add(
        "loading-screen-hidden"
    );

    setTimeout(
        () => {

            loadingScreen.hidden =
                true;

        },
        350
    );

}

function showError(error) {

    console.error(
        "Erreur pendant le démarrage d'ISLAMCRAFT :",
        error
    );

    if (loadingMessage) {

        loadingMessage.textContent =
            "Impossible de charger ISLAMCRAFT.";

    }

    if (loadingScreen) {

        loadingScreen.classList.remove(
            "loading-screen-hidden"
        );

    }

    const existing =
        document.getElementById(
            "startup-error"
        );

    if (existing) {

        existing.remove();

    }

    const errorElement =
        document.createElement(
            "p"
        );

    errorElement.id =
        "startup-error";

    errorElement.className =
        "startup-error";

    errorElement.textContent =
        error?.message ??
        "Une erreur inconnue est survenue.";

    loadingScreen
        ?.querySelector(
            ".loading-content"
        )
        ?.appendChild(
            errorElement
        );

}

async function bootstrap() {

    try {

        setLoading(
            "Initialisation du moteur...",
            10
        );

        const game =
            new Game();

        setLoading(
            "Chargement de la scène...",
            30
        );

        await game.initialize();

        setLoading(
            "Démarrage du jeu...",
            80
        );

        game.start();

        window.ISLAMCRAFT =
            game;

        setLoading(
            "ISLAMCRAFT prêt !",
            100
        );

        hideLoading();

        console.log(
            "ISLAMCRAFT client prêt."
        );

    } catch (error) {

        showError(
            error
        );

    }

}

window.addEventListener(
    "DOMContentLoaded",
    bootstrap,
    {
        once: true
    }
);
export class AnimationLoader {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.animations = new Map();

        this.loading = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AnimationLoader initialisé.");

    }

    async load(key, loader) {

        if (this.animations.has(key)) {

            return this.animations.get(key);

        }

        if (this.loading.has(key)) {

            return this.loading.get(key);

        }

        if (typeof loader !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Loader invalide pour l'animation "${key}".`

            );

        }

        const promise = Promise.resolve()

            .then(() => loader())

            .then(animation => {

                this.animations.set(

                    key,

                    animation

                );

                this.loading.delete(key);

                return animation;

            })

            .catch(error => {

                this.loading.delete(key);

                throw error;

            });

        this.loading.set(key, promise);

        return promise;

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.animations.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        return this.animations.get(key) ?? null;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.animations.delete(key);

    }

    clear() {

        this.animations.clear();

        this.loading.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.animations.size;

    }

}

    constructor(game) {

        this.assets = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AssetManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(name, asset) {

        if (!name) {

            return;

        this.assets.set(name, asset);

    }

    has(name) {

        return this.assets.has(name);

    }

    get(name) {

        return this.assets.get(name) ?? null;

    }

    remove(name) {

        this.assets.delete(name);

    }

    clear() {

        this.assets.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.assets.size;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.assets.entries()];

    }

}

export class ModelLoader {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.models = new Map();

        this.loading = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ModelLoader initialisé.");

    }

    async load(key, loader) {

        if (this.models.has(key)) {

            return this.models.get(key);

        }

        if (this.loading.has(key)) {

            return this.loading.get(key);

        }

        if (typeof loader !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Loader invalide pour le modèle "${key}".`

            );

        }

        const promise = Promise.resolve()

            .then(() => loader())

            .then(model => {

                this.models.set(key, model);

                this.loading.delete(key);

                return model;

            })

            .catch(error => {

                this.loading.delete(key);

                throw error;

            });

        this.loading.set(key, promise);

        return promise;

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.models.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        return this.models.get(key) ?? null;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.models.delete(key);

    }

    clear() {

        this.models.clear();

        this.loading.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.models.size;

    }

}
export class TextureLoader {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.textures = new Map();

        this.loading = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("TextureLoader initialisé.");

    }

    async load(key, loader) {

        if (this.textures.has(key)) {

            return this.textures.get(key);

        }

        if (this.loading.has(key)) {

            return this.loading.get(key);

        }

        if (typeof loader !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Loader invalide pour la texture "${key}".`

            );

        }

        const promise = Promise.resolve()

            .then(() => loader())

            .then(texture => {

                this.textures.set(key, texture);

                this.loading.delete(key);

                return texture;

            })

            .catch(error => {

                this.loading.delete(key);

                throw error;

            });

        this.loading.set(key, promise);

        return promise;

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.textures.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        return this.textures.get(key) ?? null;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.textures.delete(key);

    }

    clear() {

        this.textures.clear();

        this.loading.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.textures.size;

    }

}
export class AudioManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.context = null;

        this.masterVolume = 1;

        this.musicVolume = 1;

        this.sfxVolume = 1;

        this.muted = false;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        if (

            typeof window !== "undefined" &&

            window.AudioContext

        ) {

            this.context = new AudioContext();

        }

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("AudioManager initialisé.");

    }

    async resume() {

        if (

            this.context &&

            this.context.state === "suspended"

        ) {

            await this.context.resume();

        }

    }

    setMasterVolume(volume) {

        this.masterVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setMusicVolume(volume) {

        this.musicVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setSfxVolume(volume) {

        this.sfxVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setMuted(muted) {

        this.muted = Boolean(muted);

    }

    toggleMute() {

        this.muted = !this.muted;

        return this.muted;

    }

    getMasterVolume() {

        return this.masterVolume;

    }

    getMusicVolume() {

        return this.musicVolume;

    }

    getSfxVolume() {

        return this.sfxVolume;

    }

    isMuted() {

        return this.muted;

    }

    getEffectiveVolume(volume) {

        if (this.muted) {

            return 0;

        }

        return volume * this.masterVolume;

    }

    async destroy() {

        if (this.context) {

            await this.context.close();

            this.context = null;

        }

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

export class MusicManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentTrack = null;

        this.playing = false;

        this.volume = 1;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MusicManager initialisé.");

    }

    play(track) {

        if (!this.enabled || !track) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentTrack = track;

        this.playing = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-play",

            track

        );

    }

    stop() {

        if (!this.playing) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-stop",

            this.currentTrack

        );

        this.currentTrack = null;

    }

    pause() {

        if (!this.playing) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-pause",

            this.currentTrack

        );

    }

    resume() {

        if (!this.currentTrack) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-resume",

            this.currentTrack

        );

    }

    setVolume(volume) {

        this.volume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    getCurrentTrack() {

        return this.currentTrack;

    }

    getVolume() {

        return this.volume;

    }

    isPlaying() {

        return this.playing;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}
