        this.enabled = true;

    }

    disable() {

        this.enabled = false;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    getState() {
        return this.inputState;
    }

    getMovement() {

        if (!this.inputState) {
            return null;
        }

        return {
            forward:
                (this.inputState.moveForward ? 1 : 0) -
                (this.inputState.moveBackward ? 1 : 0),

            right:
                (this.inputState.moveRight ? 1 : 0) -
                (this.inputState.moveLeft ? 1 : 0)
        };
    }

    reset() {
        this.inputState?.reset?.();
    }

}
export class InputState {

    constructor() {

        this.reset();

    }

    reset() {

        this.moveForward = false;
        this.moveBackward = false;
        this.moveLeft = false;
        this.moveRight = false;

        this.jump = false;
        this.sprint = false;
        this.crouch = false;

        this.interact = false;
        this.attack = false;

        this.lookX = 0;
        this.lookY = 0;

        this.joystickX = 0;
        this.joystickY = 0;

    }

    clearFrameInput() {

        this.jump = false;
        this.interact = false;
        this.attack = false;

        this.lookX = 0;
        this.lookY = 0;

    }

    setMovement(forward, backward, left, right) {

        this.moveForward = forward;
        this.moveBackward = backward;
        this.moveLeft = left;
        this.moveRight = right;

    }

    setJoystick(x, y) {

        this.joystickX = x;
        this.joystickY = y;

    }

    setLook(x, y) {

        this.lookX = x;
        this.lookY = y;

    }

}
export class KeyboardInput {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.state = null;

        this.keys = new Set();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.state = this.game.inputState;

        window.addEventListener(

            "keydown",

            this.onKeyDown.bind(this)

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "keyup",

            this.onKeyUp.bind(this)

        );


        console.log("KeyboardInput initialisé.");

    }

    onKeyDown(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.keys.add(event.code);

        this.updateMovement();

    }

    onKeyUp(event) {

        this.keys.delete(event.code);

        this.updateMovement();

    }

    

    updateMovement() {

        this.state?.setMovement(
    this.keys.has("KeyW") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowUp"),

    this.keys.has("KeyS") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowDown"),

    this.keys.has("KeyA") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowLeft"),

    this.keys.has("KeyD") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowRight")
);

        this.state.jump = this.keys.has("Space");

        this.state.sprint =

            this.keys.has("ShiftLeft") ||

            this.keys.has("ShiftRight");

        this.state.crouch =

            this.keys.has("ControlLeft") ||

            this.keys.has("ControlRight");

        this.state.interact =

            this.keys.has("KeyE");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé pour les traitements continus

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

}
export class TouchInput {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.inputState = null;

        this.activeTouches = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.inputState = this.game.inputState;

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchstart",

            this.onTouchStart.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchmove",

            this.onTouchMove.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchend",

            this.onTouchEnd.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchcancel",

            this.onTouchEnd.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        console.log("TouchInput initialisé.");

    }

    onTouchStart(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        for (const touch of event.changedTouches) {

            this.activeTouches.set(

                touch.identifier,

                {

                    x: touch.clientX,

                    y: touch.clientY

                }

            );

        }

    }

    onTouchMove(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        for (const touch of event.changedTouches) {

            if (!this.activeTouches.has(touch.identifier)) {

                continue;

            }

            this.activeTouches.set(

                touch.identifier,

                {

                    x: touch.clientX,

                    y: touch.clientY

                }

            );

        }

    }

    onTouchEnd(event) {

        for (const touch of event.changedTouches) {

            this.activeTouches.delete(

                touch.identifier

            );

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les gestes tactiles seront interprétés
        // par TouchJoystick et CameraTouchInput.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}
export class TouchJoystick {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.inputState = null;

        this.pointerId = null;

        this.centerX = 0;

        this.centerY = 0;

        this.radius = 80;

        this.currentX = 0;

        this.currentY = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.inputState = this.game.inputState;

        console.log("TouchJoystick initialisé.");

    }

    begin(pointerId, x, y) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.pointerId = pointerId;

        this.centerX = x;

        this.centerY = y;

        this.currentX = x;

        this.currentY = y;

    }

    move(pointerId, x, y) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            pointerId !== this.pointerId

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const dx = x - this.centerX;

        const dy = y - this.centerY;

        const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

        const scale = distance > this.radius

            ? this.radius / distance

            : 1;

        const normalizedX =

            (dx * scale) / this.radius;

        const normalizedY =

            (dy * scale) / this.radius;

        this.currentX = x;

        this.currentY = y;

        this.inputState?.setJoystick(

            normalizedX,

            normalizedY

        );

    }

    end(pointerId) {

        if (pointerId !== this.pointerId) {

            return;

        }

        this.pointerId = null;

        this.inputState?.setJoystick(

            0,

            0

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé aux animations du joystick
        // et au lissage des entrées.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}
export class ClientPrediction {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.pendingInputs = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ClientPrediction initialisé.");

    }

    addInput(input) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.pendingInputs.push(input);

    }

    predict(player, deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled || !player) {

            return;

        }

        for (const input of this.pendingInputs) {

            player.applyInput?.(

                input,

                deltaTime

            );

        }

    }

    acknowledge(sequence) {

        this.pendingInputs = this.pendingInputs.filter(

            input => input.sequence > sequence

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.pendingInputs.length = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // La prédiction est exécutée
        // lors de la simulation locale.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}
export class ClientReconciliation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.lastAcknowledgedSequence = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ClientReconciliation initialisé.");

    }

    reconcile(serverState) {

        if (!this.enabled || !serverState) {

            return;

        }

        this.lastAcknowledgedSequence =

            serverState.lastProcessedInput ?? 0;

        this.game.localPlayer?.setTransform?.(

            serverState.position,

            serverState.rotation

        );

        this.game.clientPrediction?.acknowledge(

            this.lastAcknowledgedSequence

        );

    }

    getLastAcknowledgedSequence() {

        return this.lastAcknowledgedSequence;

    }

    reset() {

        this.lastAcknowledgedSequence = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les corrections sont appliquées
        // à la réception des snapshots serveur.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}
export class ConnectionManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.connected = false;

        this.connecting = false;

        this.serverUrl = "";

        this.retryCount = 0;

        this.maxRetries = 5;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ConnectionManager initialisé.");

    }

    async connect(serverUrl) {

        if (this.connected || this.connecting) {

            return;

        }

        this.serverUrl = serverUrl;

        this.connecting = true;

        try {

            await this.game.socketClient?.connect?.(

                serverUrl

            );

            this.connected = true;

            this.retryCount = 0;

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                "network:connected"

            );

        } catch (error) {

            this.retryCount++;

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                "network:connection-failed",

                error

            );

        } finally {

            this.connecting = false;

        }

    }

    disconnect() {

        this.game.socketClient?.disconnect?.();

        this.connected = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "network:disconnected"

        );

    }

    reconnect() {

        if (

            this.retryCount >= this.maxRetries ||

            !this.serverUrl

        ) {

            return;

        }

        this.connect(

            this.serverUrl

        );

    }

    isConnected() {

        return this.connected;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // heartbeat
        // reconnexion automatique
        // timeout

    }

}
export class InputBuffer {

    constructor() {

        this.inputs = [];

        this.nextSequence = 1;

        this.maxSize = 256;

    }

    add(input) {

        const bufferedInput = {

            ...input,

            sequence: this.nextSequence++

        };

        this.inputs.push(bufferedInput);

        if (this.inputs.length > this.maxSize) {

            this.inputs.shift();

        }

        return bufferedInput;

    }

    removeUntil(sequence) {

        this.inputs = this.inputs.filter(

            input => input.sequence > sequence

        );

    }

    getPendingInputs() {

        return [...this.inputs];

    }

    getLastSequence() {

        return this.nextSequence - 1;

    }

    clear() {

        this.inputs.length = 0;

        this.nextSequence = 1;

    }

    size() {

        return this.inputs.length;

    }

    isEmpty() {

        return this.inputs.length === 0;

    }

}
export class NetworkClock {

    constructor() {

        this.serverTime = 0;

        this.localTime = 0;

        this.timeOffset = 0;

        this.latency = 0;

        this.lastSyncTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("NetworkClock initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.localTime += deltaTime;

    }

    synchronize(serverTimestamp, roundTripTime = 0) {

        this.latency = roundTripTime * 0.5;

        this.serverTime = serverTimestamp;

        this.timeOffset =

            (serverTimestamp + this.latency)

            - this.localTime;

        this.lastSyncTime = this.localTime;

    }

    getServerTime() {

        return this.localTime + this.timeOffset;

    }

    getLocalTime() {

        return this.localTime;

    }

    getLatency() {

        return this.latency;

    }

    getTimeOffset() {

        return this.timeOffset;

    }

    reset() {

        this.serverTime = 0;

        this.localTime = 0;

        this.timeOffset = 0;

        this.latency = 0;

        this.lastSyncTime = 0;

    }

}
export class NetworkManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.connected = false;

        this.socketClient = null;

        this.connectionManager = null;

        this.networkClock = null;

        this.networkMetrics = null;

        this.snapshotBuffer = null;

        this.clientPrediction = null;

        this.clientReconciliation = null;

        this.remoteInterpolation = null;

        this.remoteExtrapolation = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.socketClient = this.game.socketClient;

        this.connectionManager = this.game.connectionManager;

        this.networkClock = this.game.networkClock;

        this.networkMetrics = this.game.networkMetrics;

        this.snapshotBuffer = this.game.snapshotBuffer;

        this.clientPrediction = this.game.clientPrediction;

        this.clientReconciliation = this.game.clientReconciliation;

        this.remoteInterpolation = this.game.remoteInterpolation;

        this.remoteExtrapolation = this.game.remoteExtrapolation;

        console.log("NetworkManager initialisé.");

    }

    async connect(serverUrl) {

        await this.connectionManager?.connect?.(

            serverUrl

        );

        this.connected =

            this.connectionManager?.isConnected?.() ?? false;

    }

    disconnect() {

        this.connectionManager?.disconnect?.();

        this.connected = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.connected) {

            return;

        }

        this.networkClock?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.networkMetrics?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.socketClient?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.snapshotBuffer?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.clientPrediction?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.clientReconciliation?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.remoteInterpolation?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.remoteExtrapolation?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

    }

    isConnected() {

        return this.connected;

    }

}
export class NetworkMetrics {

    constructor() {

        this.ping = 0;

        this.jitter = 0;

        this.packetLoss = 0;

        this.bytesSent = 0;

        this.bytesReceived = 0;

        this.lastPingTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("NetworkMetrics initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.lastPingTime += deltaTime;

    }

    setPing(ping) {

        this.ping = Math.max(

            0,

            ping

        );

    }

    setJitter(jitter) {

        this.jitter = Math.max(

            0,

            jitter

        );

    }

    setPacketLoss(packetLoss) {

        this.packetLoss = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                packetLoss,

                100

            )

        );

    }

    addBytesSent(bytes) {

        this.bytesSent += Math.max(

            0,

            bytes

        );

    }

    addBytesReceived(bytes) {

        this.bytesReceived += Math.max(

            0,

            bytes

        );

    }

    getStats() {

        return {

            ping: this.ping,

            jitter: this.jitter,

            packetLoss: this.packetLoss,

            bytesSent: this.bytesSent,

            bytesReceived: this.bytesReceived

        };

    }

    reset() {

        this.ping = 0;

        this.jitter = 0;

        this.packetLoss = 0;

        this.bytesSent = 0;

        this.bytesReceived = 0;

        this.lastPingTime = 0;

    }

}
export class RemoteExtrapolation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.maxExtrapolationTime = 0.25;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RemoteExtrapolation initialisé.");

    }

    extrapolate(entity, deltaTime) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !entity

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const velocity = entity.velocity;

        if (!velocity) {

            return;

        }

        const time = Math.min(

            deltaTime,

            this.maxExtrapolationTime

        );

        entity.position.x += velocity.x * time;

        entity.position.y += velocity.y * time;

        entity.position.z += velocity.z * time;

    }

    extrapolateAll(entities, deltaTime) {

        if (!Array.isArray(entities)) {

            return;

        }

        for (const entity of entities) {

            this.extrapolate(

                entity,

                deltaTime

            );

        }

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les extrapolations sont appliquées
        // après la dernière mise à jour réseau.

    }

}
export class RemoteInterpolation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.interpolationDelay = 100;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RemoteInterpolation initialisé.");

    }

    interpolate(entity, alpha) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !entity ||

            !entity.previousSnapshot ||

            !entity.nextSnapshot

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const previous = entity.previousSnapshot;

        const next = entity.nextSnapshot;

        entity.position.x = previous.position.x +

            (next.position.x - previous.position.x) * alpha;

        entity.position.y = previous.position.y +

            (next.position.y - previous.position.y) * alpha;

        entity.position.z = previous.position.z +

            (next.position.z - previous.position.z) * alpha;

        entity.rotation.x = previous.rotation.x +

            (next.rotation.x - previous.rotation.x) * alpha;

        entity.rotation.y = previous.rotation.y +

            (next.rotation.y - previous.rotation.y) * alpha;

        entity.rotation.z = previous.rotation.z +

            (next.rotation.z - previous.rotation.z) * alpha;

    }

    interpolateAll(entities, alpha) {

        if (!Array.isArray(entities)) {

            return;

        }

        for (const entity of entities) {

            this.interpolate(

                entity,

                alpha

            );

        }

    }

    setInterpolationDelay(milliseconds) {

        this.interpolationDelay = Math.max(

            0,

            milliseconds

        );

    }

    getInterpolationDelay() {

        return this.interpolationDelay;

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // L'interpolation est déclenchée
        // par NetworkManager à chaque frame.

    }

}
export class SnapshotBuffer {

    constructor() {

        this.snapshots = [];

        this.maxSnapshots = 64;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("SnapshotBuffer initialisé.");

    }

    add(snapshot) {

        if (!snapshot) {

            return;

        }

        this.snapshots.push(snapshot);

        if (this.snapshots.length > this.maxSnapshots) {

            this.snapshots.shift();

        }

    }

    getLatest() {

        if (this.snapshots.length === 0) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.snapshots[this.snapshots.length - 1];

    }

    getPrevious() {

        if (this.snapshots.length < 2) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.snapshots[this.snapshots.length - 2];

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.snapshots];

    }

    clear() {

        this.snapshots.length = 0;

    }

    size() {

        return this.snapshots.length;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les snapshots sont consommés par
        // RemoteInterpolation et
        // RemoteExtrapolation.

    }

}
export class SocketClient {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.socket = null;

        this.connected = false;

        this.serverUrl = "";

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("SocketClient initialisé.");

    }

    async connect(serverUrl) {

        if (this.connected) {

            return;

        }

        this.serverUrl = serverUrl;

        if (!serverUrl) {

            throw new Error(

                "URL du serveur manquante."

            );

        }

        if (typeof WebSocket === "undefined") {

            throw new Error(

                "WebSocket n'est pas disponible."

            );

        }

        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {

            const socket = new WebSocket(serverUrl);

            this.socket = socket;

            socket.addEventListener(

                "open",

                () => {

                    this.connected = true;

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "socket:connected"

                    );

                    resolve();

                },

                { once: true }

            );

            socket.addEventListener(

                "message",

                event => {

                    this.handleMessage(

                        event.data

                    );

                }

            );

            socket.addEventListener(

                "close",

                () => {

                    this.connected = false;

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "socket:disconnected"

                    );

                }

            );

            socket.addEventListener(

                "error",

                error => {

                    this.connected = false;

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "socket:error",

                        error

                    );

                    reject(error);

                },

                { once: true }

            );

        });

    }

    disconnect() {

        if (!this.socket) {

            return;

        }

        this.socket.close();

        this.socket = null;

        this.connected = false;

    }

    send(type, data = null) {

        if (

            !this.connected ||

            !this.socket

        ) {

            return false;

        }

        const message = JSON.stringify({

            type,

            data

        });

        this.socket.send(message);

        return true;

    }

    handleMessage(rawMessage) {

        let message;

        try {

            message = JSON.parse(rawMessage);

        } catch {

            return;

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "network:message",

            message

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // La communication est gérée
        // par les événements WebSocket.

    }

    isConnected() {

        return this.connected;

    }

}
export class ResponsiveLayout {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.width = 0;

        this.height = 0;

        this.orientation = "landscape";

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.update();

        window.addEventListener(

            "resize",

            () => this.update()

        );

        console.log("ResponsiveLayout initialisé.");

    }

    update() {

        this.width = window.innerWidth;

        this.height = window.innerHeight;

        this.orientation =

            this.width >= this.height

                ? "landscape"

                : "portrait";

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "ui:layout-changed",

            {

                width: this.width,

                height: this.height,

                orientation: this.orientation

            }

        );

    }

    getWidth() {

        return this.width;

    }

    getHeight() {

        return this.height;

    }

    getOrientation() {

        return this.orientation;

    }

    isPortrait() {

        return this.orientation === "portrait";

    }

    isLandscape() {

        return this.orientation === "landscape";

    }

}
export class UIManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.components = new Map();

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.register(

            "hud",

            this.game.hud

        );

        this.register(

            "lobby",

            this.game.lobbyUI

        );

        this.register(

            "minimap",

            this.game.minimap

        );

        this.register(

            "victory",

            this.game.victoryScreen

        );

        this.register(

            "defeat",

            this.game.defeatScreen

        );

        this.register(

            "loading",

            this.game.loadingScreen

        );

        this.register(

            "restart",

            this.game.restartScreen

        );

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("UIManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(name, component) {

        if (!name || !component) {

            return;

        }

        this.components.set(

            name,

            component

        );

    }

    unregister(name) {

        this.components.delete(name);

    }

    get(name) {

        return this.components.get(name) ?? null;

    }

    show(name, data = null) {

        const component = this.get(name);

        component?.show?.(data);

    }

    hide(name) {

        const component = this.get(name);

        component?.hide?.();

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const component of this.components.values()) {

            component?.update?.(deltaTime);

        }

    }

    showVictoryScreen(data = null) {

        this.show(

            "victory",

            data

        );

    }

    hideVictoryScreen() {

        this.hide("victory");

    }

    showDefeatScreen(data = null) {

        this.show(

            "defeat",

            data

        );

    }

    hideDefeatScreen() {

        this.hide("defeat");

    }

    showRestartScreen(data = null) {

        this.show(

            "restart",

            data

        );

    }

    hideRestartScreen() {

        this.hide("restart");

    }

}
export class LootAnimation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.active = false;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.duration = 1000;

        this.reward = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LootAnimation initialisée.");

    }

    play(reward = null) {

        this.reward = reward;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.active = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:animation-started",

            reward

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        if (this.elapsedTime >= this.duration) {

            this.finish();

        }

    }

    finish() {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

        this.active = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:animation-finished",

            this.reward

        );

    }

    stop() {

        this.active = false;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.reward = null;

    }

    isPlaying() {

        return this.active;

    }

    getProgress() {

        if (this.duration <= 0) {

            return 1;

        }

        return Math.min(

            this.elapsedTime / this.duration,

            1

        );

    }

}

export class XPBar {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.currentXP = 0;

        this.requiredXP = 100;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("XPBar initialisée.");

    }

    setXP(currentXP, requiredXP = this.requiredXP) {

        this.currentXP = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(currentXP) || 0

        );

        this.requiredXP = Math.max(

            1,

            Number(requiredXP) || 1

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "hud:xp-changed",

            {

                currentXP: this.currentXP,

                requiredXP: this.requiredXP

            }

        );

    }

    addXP(amount) {

        this.setXP(

            this.currentXP + (Number(amount) || 0),

            this.requiredXP

        );

    }

    getProgress() {

        return Math.min(

            this.currentXP / this.requiredXP,

            1

        );

    }

    getXP() {

        return this.currentXP;

    }

    getRequiredXP() {

        return this.requiredXP;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}

            ? [...players]

            : [];

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

        );

        );

    }

        if (!this.enabled) {

            .slice(0, this.maxLength);

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:username-changed",

        );

        return this.value;

        const username = this.value.trim();

        return username.length >= 3;

        this.enabled = false;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

        this.visible = false;

    }

        return this.enabled;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}

export class Minimap {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.zoom = 1;

        this.center = {

            x: 0,

            y: 0

        };

        this.markers = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("Minimap initialisée.");

    }

    setCenter(x, y) {

        this.center.x = Number(x) || 0;

        this.center.y = Number(y) || 0;

    }

    getCenter() {

        return {

            x: this.center.x,

            y: this.center.y

        };

        this.zoom = Math.max(

            0.1,

            Number(zoom) || 1

        );

    }

    getZoom() {

        return this.zoom;

    }

    setMarkers(markers) {

        this.markers = Array.isArray(markers)

            ? [...markers]

            : [];

    addMarker(marker) {

        if (!marker) {

            return;

        }

        this.markers.push(marker);

    }

    removeMarker(markerId) {

        this.markers = this.markers.filter(

            marker => marker?.id !== markerId

        );

    }

    getMarkers() {

        return [...this.markers];

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}
export class MinimapMarkers {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.markers = new Map();

        this.visible = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MinimapMarkers initialisé.");

    }

    add(marker) {

        if (!marker || marker.id === undefined) {

            return;

        }

        this.markers.set(

            marker.id,

            { ...marker }

        );

    }

    remove(markerId) {

        this.markers.delete(markerId);

    }

    update(markerId, data) {

        if (!this.markers.has(markerId)) {

            return;

        }

        const marker = this.markers.get(markerId);

        this.markers.set(

            markerId,

            {

                ...marker,

                ...data

            }

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.markers.clear();

    }

    get(markerId) {

        return this.markers.get(markerId) ?? null;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.markers.values()];

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    updateDisplay(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}
export class MinimapPlayer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.position = {

            x: 0,

            y: 0

        };

        this.rotation = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MinimapPlayer initialisé.");

    }

    setPosition(x, y) {

        this.position.x = Number(x) || 0;

        this.position.y = Number(y) || 0;

    }

    setRotation(rotation) {

        this.rotation = Number(rotation) || 0;

    }

    updateFromPlayer(player) {

        if (!player) {

            return;

        }

        if (player.position) {

            this.setPosition(

                player.position.x,

                player.position.z

            );

        }

        if (player.rotation) {

            this.setRotation(

                player.rotation.y ?? 0

            );

        }

    }

    getPosition() {

        return {

            x: this.position.x,

            y: this.position.y

        };

    }

    getRotation() {

        return this.rotation;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        this.updateFromPlayer(

            this.game.localPlayer

        );

    }

}
export class MinimapRenderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.minimap = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.minimap = this.game.minimap ?? null;

        console.log("MinimapRenderer initialisé.");

    }

    render() {

        if (!this.visible || !this.minimap) {

            return;

        }

        const markers = this.game.minimapMarkers
            ?.getAll?.() ?? [];

        const player = this.game.minimapPlayer;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "minimap:render",

            {

                center: this.minimap.getCenter?.(),

                zoom: this.minimap.getZoom?.(),

                player: player?.getPosition?.(),

                rotation: player?.getRotation?.(),

                markers

            }

        );

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.render();

    }

}

export class QuizTimer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.duration = 0;

        this.remaining = 0;

        this.running = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizTimer initialisé.");

    }

    start(duration) {

        this.duration = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(duration) || 0

        );

        this.remaining = this.duration;

        this.running = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "quiz:timer-started",

            this.remaining

        );

    }

    stop() {

        this.running = false;

    }

    reset() {

        this.remaining = this.duration;

        this.running = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.running) {

            return;

        }

        this.remaining = Math.max(

            0,

            this.remaining - deltaTime

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "quiz:timer-updated",

            this.remaining

        );

        if (this.remaining <= 0) {

            this.running = false;

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                "quiz:timer-finished"

            );

        }

    }

    getRemaining() {

        return this.remaining;

    }

    getDuration() {

        return this.duration;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

}

import { WorldSeed } from "./WorldSeed.js";
import { TerrainGenerator } from "./generation/TerrainGenerator.js";
import { StreamingRadius } from "./streaming/StreamingRadius.js";
import { ChunkManager } from "./chunks/ChunkManager.js";
import { WorldStreamer } from "./streaming/WorldStreamer.js";

export class WorldManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.seed = null;
        this.terrainGenerator = null;
        this.streamingRadius = null;
        this.chunkManager = null;
        this.streamer = null;

        this.initialized = false;
    }


    async initialize() {

        /*
         * SEED
         */

        this.seed =
            new WorldSeed(this.game);

        await this.seed.initialize();


        /*
         * GÉNÉRATION DU TERRAIN
         */

        this.terrainGenerator =
            new TerrainGenerator(this.game);

        await this.terrainGenerator.initialize();

        this.game.terrainGenerator =
            this.terrainGenerator;


        /*
         * DISTANCE DE STREAMING
         */

        this.streamingRadius =
            new StreamingRadius(this.game);

        await this.streamingRadius.initialize();

        this.game.streamingRadius =
            this.streamingRadius;


        /*
         * CHUNKS
         */

        this.chunkManager =
            new ChunkManager(this.game);

        await this.chunkManager.initialize();


        /*
         * STREAMING DU MONDE
         */

        this.streamer =
            new WorldStreamer(
                this.game,
                this.chunkManager
            );

        await this.streamer.initialize();


        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "WorldManager initialisé."
        );
    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.initialized) {
            return;
        }

        this.streamer?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

        this.chunkManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );
    }


    getSeed() {

        return this.seed?.getSeed();
    }


    getChunkManager() {

        return this.chunkManager;
    }


    getStreamer() {

        return this.streamer;
    }


    getTerrainGenerator() {

        return this.terrainGenerator;
    }


    getStreamingRadius() {

        return this.streamingRadius;
    }


    destroy() {

        this.streamer?.destroy();
        this.chunkManager?.destroy();

        if (
            this.game.terrainGenerator ===
            this.terrainGenerator
        ) {
            this.game.terrainGenerator = null;
        }

        if (
            this.game.streamingRadius ===
            this.streamingRadius
        ) {
            this.game.streamingRadius = null;
        }

        this.streamer = null;
        this.chunkManager = null;
        this.streamingRadius = null;
        this.terrainGenerator = null;
        this.seed = null;

        this.initialized = false;
    }

}
export class WorldSeed {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.seed = 0;

        this.randomState = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.generateSeed();

        console.log("WorldSeed initialisé :", this.seed);

    }

    generateSeed() {

        if (

            this.game.config.world.seed !== undefined &&

            this.game.config.world.seed !== null

        ) {

            this.seed = this.game.config.world.seed;

        }

        else {

            this.seed = Date.now();

        }

        this.randomState = this.seed;

    }

    setSeed(seed) {

        this.seed = Number(seed);

        this.randomState = this.seed;

    }

    getSeed() {

        return this.seed;

    }

    reset() {

        this.randomState = this.seed;

    }

    random() {

        this.randomState += 0x6D2B79F5;

        let t = this.randomState;

        t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);

        t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);

        return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;

    }

    randomRange(min, max) {

        return min + (max - min) * this.random();

    }

    randomInt(min, max) {

        return Math.floor(

            this.randomRange(min, max + 1)

        );

    }

    chance(probability) {

        return this.random() <= probability;

    }

}
import { DesertBiome } from "./DesertBiome.js";
import { PlainsBiome } from "./PlainsBiome.js";
import { ForestBiome } from "./ForestBiome.js";

export class BiomeManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.biomes = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        const desert = new DesertBiome(this.game);
        await desert.initialize();

        const plains = new PlainsBiome(this.game);
        await plains.initialize();

        const forest = new ForestBiome(this.game);
        await forest.initialize();

        this.biomes.set(

            desert.id,

            desert

        );

        this.biomes.set(

            plains.id,

            plains

        );

        this.biomes.set(

            forest.id,

            forest

        );

        console.log("BiomeManager initialisé.");

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.biomes.get(id);

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.biomes.has(id);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [

            ...this.biomes.values()

        ];

    }

    getBiomeData(id) {

        const biome = this.get(id);

        if (!biome) {

            return null;

        }

        return biome.getData();

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Chunk {

    constructor(game, chunkX, chunkZ) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkX = chunkX;
        this.chunkZ = chunkZ;

        this.key = `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

        this.group = new THREE.Group();

        this.group.name = this.key;

        this.group.matrixAutoUpdate = true;

        this.generated = false;
        this.loaded = false;
        this.visible = false;

        this.biome = null;

        this.terrain = null;

/*
 * Blocs supprimés manuellement par le joueur.
 *
 * La génération du terrain reste intacte.
 * Ces coordonnées permettent de mémoriser
 * les blocs cassés.
 */
this.removedBlocks = new Set();

this.placedBlocks = new Map();

this.entities = [];

        this.objects = [];

        this.structures = [];

        this.meshes = [];

        this.boundingBox = new THREE.Box3();

        this.center = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.size =
            this.game.config.world.chunkSize;

        this.computeWorldPosition();

    }

    computeWorldPosition() {

        const worldX = this.chunkX * this.size;
        const worldZ = this.chunkZ * this.size;

        this.group.position.set(

            worldX,

            0,

            worldZ

        );

        this.center.set(

            worldX + this.size * 0.5,

            0,

            worldZ + this.size * 0.5

        );

    }

    add(object) {

        this.group.add(object);

        this.objects.push(object);

    }

    remove(object) {

        this.group.remove(object);

        this.objects = this.objects.filter(

            o => o !== object

        );

    }

    addEntity(entity) {

        this.entities.push(entity);

    }

    removeEntity(entity) {

        this.entities = this.entities.filter(

            e => e !== entity

        );

    }

    addMesh(mesh) {

        this.meshes.push(mesh);

        this.group.add(mesh);

    }

    getBlockKey(localX, localY, localZ) {

    return `${localX}:${localY}:${localZ}`;

}


isBlockRemoved(localX, localY, localZ) {

    return this.removedBlocks.has(
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        )
    );

}


getHighestSolidY(localX, localZ) {

    if (
        !Array.isArray(this.terrain)
    ) {

        return -1;

    }


    const rawHeight =
        Number(
            this.terrain[localZ]?.[localX]
        );


    let highestY =
        Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
            ? Math.floor(rawHeight)
            : -1;


    /*
     * Les blocs construits peuvent
     * dépasser la hauteur originale
     * de la colonne.
     */

    for (
        const key of this.placedBlocks.keys()
    ) {

        const parts =
            key.split(":").map(Number);

        if (parts.length !== 3) {

            continue;

        }


        const [
            x,
            y,
            z
        ] = parts;


        if (
            x === localX &&
            z === localZ &&
            y > highestY
        ) {

            highestY = y;

        }

    }


    /*
     * Chercher le bloc réellement
     * le plus haut qui existe encore.
     */

    for (
        let y = highestY;
        y >= 0;
        y--
    ) {

        const key =
            this.getBlockKey(
                localX,
                y,
                localZ
            );


        if (
            this.removedBlocks.has(key)
        ) {

            continue;

        }


        if (
            this.placedBlocks.has(key)
        ) {

            return y;

        }


        if (
            Number.isFinite(rawHeight) &&
            y <= Math.floor(rawHeight)
        ) {

            return y;

        }

    }


    return -1;

}


getBlockType(localX, localY, localZ) {

    if (
        !Number.isInteger(localX) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localY) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localZ)
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    if (
        localX < 0 ||
        localX >= this.size ||
        localZ < 0 ||
        localZ >= this.size ||
        localY < 0
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const key =
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        );


    /*
     * Un bloc supprimé n'existe plus.
     */

    if (
        this.removedBlocks.has(key)
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    /*
     * Un bloc construit par le joueur
     * garde exactement son type.
     */

    if (
        this.placedBlocks.has(key)
    ) {

        return this.placedBlocks.get(key);

    }


    const rawHeight =
        Number(
            this.terrain?.[localZ]?.[localX]
        );


    if (
        !Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const topY =
        Math.floor(rawHeight);


    /*
     * Aucun bloc généré au-dessus
     * de la hauteur originale.
     */

    if (
        localY > topY
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const highestY =
        this.getHighestSolidY(
            localX,
            localZ
        );


    if (
        localY === highestY
    ) {

        return "grass";

    }


    if (
        localY >= highestY - 3
    ) {

        return "dirt";

    }


    return "stone";

}


setPlacedBlock(
    localX,
    localY,
    localZ,
    blockType
) {

    if (
        !Number.isInteger(localX) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localY) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localZ)
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    if (
        localX < 0 ||
        localX >= this.size ||
        localY < 0 ||
        localY >= (
            this.game.config?.world?.worldHeight
            ?? 128
        ) ||
        localZ < 0 ||
        localZ >= this.size
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const validTypes = [
        "grass",
        "dirt",
        "stone"
    ];


    if (
        !validTypes.includes(blockType)
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const key =
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        );


    this.placedBlocks.set(
        key,
        blockType
    );


    /*
     * Si cet emplacement avait été
     * supprimé auparavant, le nouveau
     * bloc le réactive.
     */

    this.removedBlocks.delete(key);


    return true;

}


removeBlock(
    localX,
    localY,
    localZ
) {

    if (
        localX < 0 ||
        localX >= this.size ||
        localY < 0 ||
        localZ < 0 ||
        localZ >= this.size
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const key =
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        );


    /*
     * Un bloc construit est retiré
     * de la carte des blocs construits.
     */

    this.placedBlocks.delete(key);


    /*
     * Un bloc généré est mémorisé
     * comme supprimé.
     */

    this.removedBlocks.add(key);


    return true;

}

    setBiome(biome) {

        this.biome = biome;

    }

    markGenerated() {

        this.generated = true;

    }

    markLoaded() {

        this.loaded = true;

    }

    setVisible(value) {

        this.visible = value;

        this.group.visible = value;

    }

    updateBoundingBox() {

        this.boundingBox.setFromObject(

            this.group

        );

    }

    getWorldPosition() {

        return this.group.position;

    }

    destroy() {

    for (const child of this.group.children) {

        if (child.geometry) {

            child.geometry.dispose();

        }

        if (child.material) {

            if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                child.material.forEach(
                    material => material.dispose()
                );

            }
            else {

                child.material.dispose();

            }

        }

    }

    this.group.clear();

    this.objects.length = 0;

    this.structures.length = 0;

    this.entities.length = 0;

    this.meshes.length = 0;

    this.terrain = null;

    this.biome = null;

    this.generated = false;

    this.loaded = false;

    this.visible = false;

}

}
export class ChunkCache {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.cache = new Map();

        const configuredMax =
            this.game.config?.world?.maxCachedChunks;

        this.maxCachedChunks =
            Number.isFinite(configuredMax)
                ? Math.max(
                    0,
                    Math.floor(configuredMax)
                )
                : 512;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "ChunkCache initialisé."
        );

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.cache.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        const entry =
            this.cache.get(key);

        if (!entry) {

            return null;

        }

        entry.lastAccess =
            performance.now();

        return entry.chunk;

    }

    save(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !chunk.key) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Un chunk déjà détruit ne doit jamais
         * entrer dans le cache.
         */

        if (
            !chunk.group ||
            !chunk.terrain
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Si le cache est désactivé,
         * ne rien conserver.
         */

        if (
            this.maxCachedChunks <= 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Retirer une ancienne entrée avant
         * de remplacer le chunk.
         */

        this.cache.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

        this.cache.set(
            chunk.key,
            {
                chunk,
                lastAccess:
                    performance.now()
            }
        );

        this.cleanup();

    }

    touch(key) {

        const entry =
            this.cache.get(key);

        if (!entry) {

            return;

        }

        entry.lastAccess =
            performance.now();

    }

    restore(key) {

        const entry =
            this.cache.get(key);

        if (!entry) {

            return null;

        }

        /*
         * Le chunk doit encore être
         * utilisable.
         */

        if (
            !entry.chunk ||
            !entry.chunk.group ||
            !entry.chunk.terrain
        ) {

            this.cache.delete(key);

            return null;

        }

        entry.lastAccess =
            performance.now();

        return entry.chunk;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.cache.delete(
            key
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.cache.clear();

    }

    cleanup() {

        if (
            this.cache.size <=
            this.maxCachedChunks
        ) {

            return;

        }

        const entries =
            [...this.cache.entries()];

        entries.sort(
            (a, b) =>
                a[1].lastAccess -
                b[1].lastAccess
        );

        while (
            this.cache.size >
            this.maxCachedChunks
        ) {

            const oldest =
                entries.shift();

            if (!oldest) {

                break;

            }

            this.cache.delete(
                oldest[0]
            );

        }

    }

}
export class ChunkCoordinates {

    static getChunkSize(game) {

        return game.config.world.chunkSize;

    }

    static worldToChunk(game, x, z) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        return {

            chunkX: Math.floor(x / size),

            chunkZ: Math.floor(z / size)

        };

    }

    static chunkToWorld(game, chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        return {

            x: chunkX * size,

            z: chunkZ * size

        };

    }

    static getChunkKey(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        return `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

    }

    static parseChunkKey(key) {

        const [x, z] = key.split(":").map(Number);

        return {

            chunkX: x,

            chunkZ: z

        };

    }

    static localPosition(game, x, z) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        const chunk = this.worldToChunk(game, x, z);

        return {

            chunkX: chunk.chunkX,

            chunkZ: chunk.chunkZ,

            localX: x - chunk.chunkX * size,

            localZ: z - chunk.chunkZ * size

        };

    }

    static worldPosition(game, chunkX, chunkZ, localX, localZ) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        return {

            x: chunkX * size + localX,

            z: chunkZ * size + localZ

        };

    }

    static distance(chunkAX, chunkAZ, chunkBX, chunkBZ) {

        return Math.max(

            Math.abs(chunkAX - chunkBX),

            Math.abs(chunkAZ - chunkBZ)

        );

    }

    static insideRadius(

        chunkAX,

        chunkAZ,

        chunkBX,

        chunkBZ,

        radius

    ) {

        return (

            this.distance(

                chunkAX,

                chunkAZ,

                chunkBX,

                chunkBZ

            ) <= radius

        );

    }

}
import { ChunkRenderer } from "./ChunkRenderer.js";

export class ChunkLoader {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager;

        this.renderer = null;

        this.loadingQueue = [];

        this.queued = new Set();

        this.loading = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.renderer =
            new ChunkRenderer(
                this.game
            );

        await this.renderer.initialize();

        console.log(
            "ChunkLoader initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        this.processQueue();

    }

    loadChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

        if (
            this.chunkManager.hasChunk(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            )
        ) {

            return this.chunkManager.getChunk(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        }

        if (
            this.queued.has(key)
        ) {

            return null;

        }

        this.queued.add(key);

this.loadingQueue.push({
    chunkX,
    chunkZ,
    key
});

return null;

    }

    processQueue() {

        if (this.loading) {

            return;

        }

        if (this.loadingQueue.length === 0) {

            return;

        }

        this.loading = true;

        const request =
            this.loadingQueue.shift();

        this.queued.delete(
            request.key
        );

        try {

            const chunk =
                this.chunkManager.createChunk(
                    request.chunkX,
                    request.chunkZ
                );

            if (!chunk) {

                return;

            }

            /*
             * Générer le chunk s'il ne l'est pas encore.
             */

            if (!chunk.generated) {

                const terrainGenerator =
                    this.game.terrainGenerator ??
                    this.game.world?.terrainGenerator ??
                    this.game.worldManager?.terrainGenerator;

                if (
                    terrainGenerator &&
                    typeof terrainGenerator.generate === "function"
                ) {

                    terrainGenerator.generate(
                        chunk
                    );

                }
                else {

                    console.warn(
                        "TerrainGenerator introuvable."
                    );

                }

            }

            /*
             * Le générateur utilise chunk.generated
             * comme indicateur de réussite.
             */

            if (!chunk.generated) {

                console.warn(
                    `Échec génération chunk ${chunk.key}.`
                );

            }

            /*
             * Générer l'environnement uniquement
             * après génération réussie.
             */

            if (chunk.generated) {

                const environmentManager =
                    this.game.environmentManager ??
                    this.game.world?.environmentManager ??
                    this.game.worldManager?.environmentManager;

                if (
                    environmentManager &&
                    typeof environmentManager.generateChunk === "function"
                ) {

                    environmentManager.generateChunk(
                        chunk
                    );

                }

            }

            /*
             * Ajouter le chunk à la scène.
             */

            if (chunk.generated) {

    this.renderer.render(
        chunk
    );


    /*
     * POSITIONNEMENT INITIAL DU JOUEUR
     *
     * On ne positionne le joueur que lorsque
     * le chunk central est réellement généré.
     */

    const player =
        this.game.localPlayer;

    if (
        player &&
        request.chunkX === 0 &&
        request.chunkZ === 0 &&
        Array.isArray(chunk.terrain)
    ) {

        const spawnX = 0;
        const spawnZ = 0;

        const terrainHeight =
            Number(
                chunk.terrain[spawnZ]?.[spawnX]
            );

        if (
            Number.isFinite(
                terrainHeight
            )
        ) {

            const spawnY =
                Math.floor(
                    terrainHeight
                ) + 0.1;

            player.setPosition(
                spawnX,
                spawnY,
                spawnZ
            );

            console.log(
                "Spawn joueur :",
                spawnX,
                spawnY,
                spawnZ
            );
        }
    }
}

        }
        catch (error) {

            console.error(
                `Erreur lors du chargement du chunk ${request.key}:`,
                error
            );

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(
                "world:chunk-load-error",
                {
                    chunkX: request.chunkX,
                    chunkZ: request.chunkZ,
                    error
                }
            );

        }
        finally {
    this.loading = false;
}

    }

    clearQueue() {

        this.loadingQueue.length = 0;

        this.queued.clear();

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clearQueue();

        this.loading = false;

        this.renderer?.clear?.();

        this.renderer = null;

    }

}
import { Chunk } from "./Chunk.js";
import { ChunkLoader } from "./ChunkLoader.js";
import { ChunkUnloader } from "./ChunkUnloader.js";
import { ChunkCache } from "./ChunkCache.js";

export class ChunkManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunks = new Map();

        this.loader = null;

        this.unloader = null;

        this.cache = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.cache =
            new ChunkCache(this.game);

        await this.cache.initialize();

        this.loader =
            new ChunkLoader(
                this.game,
                this
            );

        await this.loader.initialize();

        this.unloader =
            new ChunkUnloader(
                this.game,
                this
            );

        await this.unloader.initialize();

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "ChunkManager initialisé."
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.initialized) {
            return;
        }

        this.loader?.update(deltaTime);

        this.unloader?.update(deltaTime);

    }

    getKey(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        return `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

    }

    hasChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            typeof chunkX === "string"
                ? chunkX
                : this.getKey(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                );

        return this.chunks.has(key);

    }

    getChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            typeof chunkX === "string"
                ? chunkX
                : this.getKey(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                );

        return this.chunks.get(key);

    }

    createChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            this.getKey(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        /*
         * Le chunk est déjà chargé.
         */

        if (
            this.chunks.has(key)
        ) {

            return this.chunks.get(key);

        }

        /*
         * Le chunk existe dans le cache.
         * On le récupère au lieu d'en créer
         * un nouveau.
         */

        if (
            this.cache?.has(key)
        ) {

            const cachedChunk =
                this.cache.restore(key);

            if (cachedChunk) {

                this.chunks.set(
                    key,
                    cachedChunk
                );

                return cachedChunk;

            }

        }

        /*
         * Création normale du chunk.
         */

        const chunk =
            new Chunk(
                this.game,
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        this.chunks.set(
            key,
            chunk
        );

        return chunk;

    }

    loadChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        if (!this.loader) {
            return null;
        }

        return this.loader.loadChunk(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

    }

    registerChunk(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        this.chunks.set(
            chunk.key,
            chunk
        );

    }

    removeChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            typeof chunkX === "string"
                ? chunkX
                : this.getKey(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                );

        const chunk =
            this.chunks.get(key);

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        /*
         * Retirer le chunk de la scène.
         */

        this.loader?.renderer?.remove?.(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Sauvegarder le chunk dans le cache
         * avant de le supprimer.
         */

        if (this.cache) {

            this.cache.save(
                chunk
            );

        }

        /*
         * Détruire le chunk.
         */

        if (
            typeof chunk.destroy === "function"
        ) {

            chunk.destroy();

        }

        /*
         * Retirer le chunk de la liste
         * des chunks actuellement chargés.
         */

        this.chunks.delete(key);

    }

    getLoadedChunks() {

        return this.chunks;

    }

    getChunkCount() {

        return this.chunks.size;

    }

    clear() {

        for (
            const chunk of this.chunks.values()
        ) {

            /*
             * Retirer le chunk de la scène.
             */

            this.loader?.renderer?.remove?.(
                chunk
            );

            /*
             * Détruire le chunk.
             */

            if (
                typeof chunk.destroy === "function"
            ) {

                chunk.destroy();

            }

        }

        this.chunks.clear();

    }

    rebuildChunk(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }


    this.loader?.renderer?.rebuild?.(
        chunk
    );

}

    destroy() {

        this.loader?.destroy();

        this.unloader?.destroy();

        this.clear();

        this.loader = null;

        this.unloader = null;

        this.cache?.clear?.();

        this.cache = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}
import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChunkRenderer {

    createTerrainMesh(chunk) {

    if (
        !chunk ||
        !chunk.terrain ||
        chunk.terrain.length === 0
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const size =
        chunk.size;


    let grassCount = 0;
    let dirtCount = 0;
    let stoneCount = 0;


    /*
     * Trouver la hauteur maximale
     * nécessaire, y compris les blocs
     * construits par le joueur.
     */

    let maxY = 0;


    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            const rawHeight =
                Number(
                    chunk.terrain[localZ]?.[localX]
                );


            if (
                Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
            ) {

                maxY =
                    Math.max(
                        maxY,
                        Math.floor(rawHeight)
                    );

            }

        }

    }


    /*
     * Les blocs placés peuvent être
     * plus hauts que le terrain original.
     */

    for (
        const key of chunk.placedBlocks.keys()
    ) {

        const parts =
            key.split(":").map(Number);


        if (
            parts.length !== 3
        ) {

            continue;

        }


        maxY =
            Math.max(
                maxY,
                parts[1]
            );

    }


    /*
     * Première passe :
     * compter chaque type de bloc.
     */

    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            for (
                let y = 0;
                y <= maxY;
                y++
            ) {

                const blockType =
                    chunk.getBlockType(
                        localX,
                        y,
                        localZ
                    );


                if (
                    blockType === "grass"
                ) {

                    grassCount++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "dirt"
                ) {

                    dirtCount++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "stone"
                ) {

                    stoneCount++;

                }

            }

        }

    }


    const group =
        new THREE.Group();


    group.name =
        `terrain_${chunk.key}`;


    const blockGeometry =
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(
            1,
            1,
            1
        );


    const grassMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x6ea84a,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    const dirtMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x8d6e63,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    const stoneMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x777777,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    const grassMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            grassMaterial,
            Math.max(1, grassCount)
        );


    const dirtMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            dirtMaterial,
            Math.max(1, dirtCount)
        );


    const stoneMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            stoneMaterial,
            Math.max(1, stoneCount)
        );


    grassMesh.castShadow = true;
    grassMesh.receiveShadow = true;

    dirtMesh.castShadow = true;
    dirtMesh.receiveShadow = true;

    stoneMesh.castShadow = true;
    stoneMesh.receiveShadow = true;


    const matrix =
        new THREE.Matrix4();


    let grassIndex = 0;
    let dirtIndex = 0;
    let stoneIndex = 0;


    /*
     * Deuxième passe :
     * placer chaque bloc selon
     * son véritable type.
     */

    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            for (
                let y = 0;
                y <= maxY;
                y++
            ) {

                const blockType =
                    chunk.getBlockType(
                        localX,
                        y,
                        localZ
                    );


                if (!blockType) {

                    continue;

                }


                matrix.makeTranslation(
                    localX + 0.5,
                    y + 0.5,
                    localZ + 0.5
                );


                if (
                    blockType === "grass"
                ) {

                    grassMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        grassIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    grassIndex++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "dirt"
                ) {

                    dirtMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        dirtIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    dirtIndex++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "stone"
                ) {

                    stoneMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        stoneIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    stoneIndex++;

                }

            }

        }

    }


    grassMesh.count =
        grassIndex;

    dirtMesh.count =
        dirtIndex;

    stoneMesh.count =
        stoneIndex;


    grassMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;

    dirtMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;

    stoneMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;


    grassMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "grass",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    dirtMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "dirt",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    stoneMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "stone",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    if (grassIndex > 0) {

        group.add(
            grassMesh
        );

    }


    if (dirtIndex > 0) {

        group.add(
            dirtMesh
        );

    }


    if (stoneIndex > 0) {

        group.add(
            stoneMesh
        );

    }


    group.userData = {
        type: "voxelTerrain",
        chunkKey: chunk.key,
        size
    };


    return group;

}

getBiomeColor(biome) {

    switch (biome) {

        case "desert":

            return 0xd9c27a;

        case "forest":

            return 0x3f8c3a;

        case "plains":

            return 0x6ea84a;

        default:

            return 0x6ea84a;

    }

}

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.renderedChunks = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene =
            this.game.scene.getScene();

        console.log(
            "ChunkRenderer initialisé."
        );

    }

    render(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }

    if (
        this.renderedChunks.has(
            chunk.key
        )
    ) {

        return;

    }

    if (
        chunk.terrain &&
        chunk.meshes.length === 0
    ) {

        const terrainMesh =
            this.createTerrainMesh(
                chunk
            );

        if (terrainMesh) {

            chunk.addMesh(
                terrainMesh
            );

        }

    }

    this.scene.add(
        chunk.group
    );

    chunk.setVisible(true);

    chunk.markLoaded();

    this.renderedChunks.set(
        chunk.key,
        chunk
    );

}
rebuild(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }


    /*
     * Supprimer l'ancien mesh du terrain.
     */

    for (
        const mesh of chunk.meshes
    ) {

        chunk.group.remove(mesh);


        mesh.traverse?.(
            object => {

                if (object.geometry) {

                    object.geometry.dispose();

                }


                if (object.material) {

                    if (
                        Array.isArray(
                            object.material
                        )
                    ) {

                        object.material.forEach(
                            material => {

                                material.dispose();

                            }
                        );

                    }
                    else {

                        object.material.dispose();

                    }

                }

            }
        );

    }


    chunk.meshes.length = 0;


    /*
     * Recréer le terrain avec les
     * blocs actuellement présents.
     */

    const terrainMesh =
        this.createTerrainMesh(
            chunk
        );


    if (terrainMesh) {

        chunk.addMesh(
            terrainMesh
        );

    }

}

    hide(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        chunk.setVisible(false);

    }

    show(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        chunk.setVisible(true);

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        if (
            !this.renderedChunks.has(
                chunk.key
            )
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.scene.remove(
            chunk.group
        );

        this.renderedChunks.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    isRendered(chunk) {

        return this.renderedChunks.has(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    clear() {

        if (!this.scene) {
            return;
        }

        for (
            const chunk of
            this.renderedChunks.values()
        ) {

            this.scene.remove(
                chunk.group
            );

        }

        this.renderedChunks.clear();

    }

    update() {

        // Réservé aux futures optimisations :
        // - GPU Culling
        // - Occlusion Culling
        // - Distance Rendering
        // - Instancing dynamique

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clear();

        this.scene = null;

        this.game = null;

    }

}

export class ChunkUnloader {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "ChunkUnloader initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        const player =
            this.game.localPlayer;

        if (!player) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize;

        const unloadDistance =
            this.game.config?.world?.unloadDistance;

        /*
         * Vérifier la configuration.
         */

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(unloadDistance) ||
            unloadDistance < 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Calculer le chunk dans lequel
         * se trouve actuellement le joueur.
         */

        const playerChunkX =
            Math.floor(
                player.position.x / chunkSize
            );

        const playerChunkZ =
            Math.floor(
                player.position.z / chunkSize
            );

        /*
         * Récupérer les chunks actuellement
         * chargés.
         */

        const loadedChunks =
            this.chunkManager.getLoadedChunks();

        /*
         * Créer une liste des chunks à retirer.
         *
         * On ne les supprime pas directement
         * pendant le parcours de la Map.
         */

        const chunksToRemove = [];

        for (
            const chunk of loadedChunks.values()
        ) {

            if (!chunk) {
                continue;
            }

            const dx =
                chunk.chunkX -
                playerChunkX;

            const dz =
                chunk.chunkZ -
                playerChunkZ;

            /*
             * Distance en grille.
             *
             * Exemple :
             *
             * joueur = 0:0
             * chunk  = 0:5
             *
             * distance = 5
             */

            const distance =
                Math.max(
                    Math.abs(dx),
                    Math.abs(dz)
                );

            /*
             * Le chunk est encore assez proche.
             */

            if (
                distance <= unloadDistance
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            chunksToRemove.push({
                chunkX: chunk.chunkX,
